-- ================== WHITELIST ==================
local WHITELIST = {
    ["renegado6661l"]         = { Role = "DONO",     Permanent = true },
    ["PREENCHA_AQUI_SUBDONO"] = { Role = "SUB DONO", Days = 30 },
    ["PREENCHA_AQUI_ADM_1"]   = { Role = "ADM",      Days = 30 },
    ["PREENCHA_AQUI_ADM_2"]   = { Role = "ADM",      Days = 30 },
    ["PREENCHA_AQUI_ADM_3"]   = { Role = "ADM",      Days = 30 },
    ["PREENCHA_AQUI_ADM_4"]   = { Role = "ADM",      Days = 30 },
    ["PREENCHA_AQUI_ADM_5"]   = { Role = "ADM",      Days = 30 },

    ["PREENCHA_AQUI_ADM_7"]   = { Role = "ADM",      Days = 30 },
}

local NETWORK_ROLES = {}
local REQUIRE_WHITELIST = true -- true = só obedece comandos de quem está na WHITELIST (evita estranhos usando ;KICK ALL etc.)
local IS_ADMIN, MY_ROLE = false, "USER"
do
    local entry = WHITELIST[LocalPlayer.Name]
    if entry then
        if entry.Permanent then
            IS_ADMIN, MY_ROLE = true, entry.Role
        elseif entry.Days then
            local ok, log = pcall(function()
                return game:GetService("DataStoreService"):GetDataStore("F1L_Log"):GetAsync("log")
            end)
            log = log or {}
            local now = os.time()
            if not log[LocalPlayer.Name] then
                log[LocalPlayer.Name] = now
                pcall(function()
                    game:GetService("DataStoreService"):GetDataStore("F1L_Log"):SetAsync("log", log)
                end)
                IS_ADMIN, MY_ROLE = true, entry.Role
            elseif (now - log[LocalPlayer.Name]) / 86400 <= entry.Days then
                IS_ADMIN, MY_ROLE = true, entry.Role
            end
        end
    end
end
print("[Fantasma1l Hub] Modo: " .. (IS_ADMIN and ("ADMIN - " .. MY_ROLE) or "Brookhaven Only"))

pcall(function()
    StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {
        Title = "Fantasma1l Hub",
        Text = IS_ADMIN and (MY_ROLE) or "Brookhaven RP Edition",
        Duration = 5
    })
end)

-- ================== HELPERS ==================
local function notify(t, txt)
    pcall(function() StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", { Title = t, Text = txt, Duration = 5 }) end)
end

local function getRealImageIdDelta(id)
    local cleanId = tostring(id):match("%d+")
    if not cleanId then return "rbxassetid://" .. id end
    return "rbxassetid://" .. cleanId
end

local function safePlayAnimation(character, animId)
    if not character then return nil end
    local hum = character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
    local anim = hum and hum:FindFirstChild("Animator")
    if not anim then return nil end
    local a = Instance.new("Animation")
    a.AnimationId = animId
    local ok, track = pcall(function() return anim:LoadAnimation(a) end)
    if ok and track then track:Play() return track end
    return nil
end

local function fetchAssetAsModel(id)
    local s, objs = pcall(function() return game:GetObjects("rbxassetid://" .. tostring(id)) end)
    if s and objs and #objs > 0 then
        local m = Instance.new("Model")
        for _, o in ipairs(objs) do o.Parent = m end
        if not m.PrimaryPart then m.PrimaryPart = m:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true) end
        return m
    end
    return nil
end

local function makeModelFallSlowly(model)
    task.spawn(function()
        while model and model.Parent do
            for _, p in ipairs(model:GetDescendants()) do
                if p:IsA("BasePart") and not p.Anchored then
                    p.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, -7, 0)
                end
            end
            task.wait(0.1)
        end
    end)
end

local function sendNetCmd(cmd)
    pcall(function()
        if TextChatService.ChatVersion == Enum.ChatVersion.TextChatService then
            local ch = TextChatService:FindFirstChild("TextChannels") and TextChatService.TextChannels:FindFirstChild("RBXGeneral")
            if ch then ch:SendAsync(cmd) end
        else
            local sayReq = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("DefaultChatSystemChatEvents")
                and ReplicatedStorage.DefaultChatSystemChatEvents:FindFirstChild("SayMessageRequest")
            if sayReq then sayReq:FireServer(cmd, "All") end
        end
    end)
end

-- ================== TAG ==================
function applyOverheadTag(player)
    pcall(function()
        if not player or not player.Character then return end
        local head = player.Character:WaitForChild("Head", 3)
        if not head then return end
        if head:FindFirstChild("FantasmaOverhead") then head.FantasmaOverhead:Destroy() end

        local cargo = "USUARIO"
        if WHITELIST[player.Name] then cargo = WHITELIST[player.Name].Role
        elseif NETWORK_ROLES[player.Name] then cargo = NETWORK_ROLES[player.Name] end
        if player == LocalPlayer then cargo = cargo .. " (VOCÊ)" end

        local bb = Instance.new("BillboardGui", head)
        bb.Name, bb.Size, bb.StudsOffset, bb.AlwaysOnTop = "FantasmaOverhead", UDim2.new(0, 240, 0, 50), Vector3.new(0, 3, 0), true

        local fr = Instance.new("Frame", bb)
        fr.Size, fr.BackgroundColor3 = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0), Color3.fromRGB(15, 12, 22)
        fr.BackgroundTransparency, fr.BorderSizePixel = 0.25, 0
        Instance.new("UICorner", fr).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)

        local st = Instance.new("UIStroke", fr)
        st.Color, st.Thickness = Color3.fromRGB(168, 85, 247), 1.8

        local ic = Instance.new("ImageLabel", fr)
        ic.Size, ic.Position, ic.BackgroundTransparency, ic.Image = UDim2.new(0, 32, 0, 32), UDim2.new(0, 8, 0.5, -16), 1, ICON_ID

        local lbl = Instance.new("TextLabel", fr)
        lbl.Size, lbl.Position, lbl.BackgroundTransparency = UDim2.new(1, -50, 1, 0), UDim2.new(0, 45, 0, 0), 1
        lbl.Text = "FANTASMA1L | " .. cargo
        lbl.TextColor3, lbl.TextSize = Color3.fromRGB(220, 200, 255), 13
        lbl.Font, lbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.Font.GothamBold, Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    end)
end

local function setupPlayerTag(plr)
    plr.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
        task.wait(1)
        if plr == LocalPlayer then applyOverheadTag(plr)
        elseif IS_ADMIN and activeScriptUsers[plr.Name] then applyOverheadTag(plr) end
    end)
    if plr.Character then
        if plr == LocalPlayer then applyOverheadTag(plr)
        elseif IS_ADMIN and activeScriptUsers[plr.Name] then applyOverheadTag(plr) end
    end
end
setupPlayerTag(LocalPlayer)

if IS_ADMIN then
    for _, p in pairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if p ~= LocalPlayer then
            p.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
                task.wait(1.5)
                if activeScriptUsers[p.Name] then applyOverheadTag(p) end
            end)
            if p.Character and activeScriptUsers[p.Name] then applyOverheadTag(p) end
        end
    end
    Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(function(p)
        if p ~= LocalPlayer then
            p.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
                task.wait(1.5)
                if activeScriptUsers[p.Name] then applyOverheadTag(p) end
            end)
        end
    end)
end

-- ================== KAMUI ==================
local function toggleKamuiDimensionLocal(state)
    kamuiEnabled = state
    if kamuiEnabled then
        Lighting.FogColor = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
        Lighting.FogStart, Lighting.FogEnd = 0, 300
        Lighting.Ambient, Lighting.OutdoorAmbient = Color3.fromRGB(15, 15, 20), Color3.fromRGB(15, 15, 20)

        kamuiFolder = Instance.new("Folder", workspace)
        kamuiFolder.Name = "KamuiDimensionBox"

        local c = Vector3.new(0, 500, 0)
        local hx, hy, hz = kamuiSize.X / 2, kamuiSize.Y / 2, kamuiSize.Z / 2
        local wt = 12

        local function wall(sz, pos)
            local p = Instance.new("Part", kamuiFolder)
            p.Size, p.Position, p.Anchored, p.CanCollide, p.CastShadow = sz, c + pos, true, true, false
            p.Material, p.Color = Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic, Color3.fromRGB(10, 10, 10)
        end
        wall(Vector3.new(kamuiSize.X, wt, kamuiSize.Z), Vector3.new(0, -hy, 0))
        wall(Vector3.new(kamuiSize.X, wt, kamuiSize.Z), Vector3.new(0, hy, 0))
        wall(Vector3.new(wt, kamuiSize.Y, kamuiSize.Z), Vector3.new(-hx, 0, 0))
        wall(Vector3.new(wt, kamuiSize.Y, kamuiSize.Z), Vector3.new(hx, 0, 0))
        wall(Vector3.new(kamuiSize.X, kamuiSize.Y, wt), Vector3.new(0, 0, -hz))
        wall(Vector3.new(kamuiSize.X, kamuiSize.Y, wt), Vector3.new(0, 0, hz))

        local cx, cz = hx - 15, hz - 15
        local lh = kamuiSize.Y - 40
        for _, pos in ipairs({Vector3.new(-cx,0,-cz), Vector3.new(cx,0,-cz), Vector3.new(-cx,0,cz), Vector3.new(cx,0,cz)}) do
            local l = Instance.new("Part", kamuiFolder)
            l.Size, l.Position, l.Anchored, l.CanCollide = Vector3.new(6, lh, 6), c + pos, true, false
            l.Material, l.Color = Enum.Material.Neon, Color3.fromRGB(200, 220, 255)
            local pl = Instance.new("PointLight", l)
            pl.Color, pl.Range, pl.Brightness = Color3.fromRGB(200, 220, 255), 250, 3
        end

        local bs, sp, gr = 18, 6, 12
        local fy = c.Y - hy + wt / 2
        for x = -gr, gr do
            for z = -gr, gr do
                if math.abs(x) > 2 or math.abs(z) > 2 then
                    local h = math.random(25, 140)
                    local b = Instance.new("Part", kamuiFolder)
                    b.Size = Vector3.new(bs, h, bs)
                    b.Position = Vector3.new(c.X + x * (bs + sp), fy + h / 2, c.Z + z * (bs + sp))
                    b.Anchored, b.Material, b.Color = true, Enum.Material.Concrete, Color3.fromRGB(115, 125, 135)
                end
            end
        end

        local pf = Instance.new("Part", kamuiFolder)
        pf.Name, pf.Size = "KamuiTargetPlatform", Vector3.new(70, 4, 70)
        pf.Position, pf.Anchored = Vector3.new(c.X, fy + 85, c.Z), true
        pf.Material, pf.Color = Enum.Material.Slate, Color3.fromRGB(190, 200, 210)

        task.spawn(function()
            task.wait(0.1)
            local chr = LocalPlayer.Character
            local h = chr and chr:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if h then h.CFrame = pf.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, 6, 0) end
        end)
        notify("Kamui", "Dimensão gerada com sucesso!")
    else
        if kamuiFolder then kamuiFolder:Destroy() kamuiFolder = nil end
        Lighting.FogEnd = 100000
        notify("Kamui", "Dimensão fechada!")
    end
end

-- ================== THE LIFEFORM ==================
local function spawnLifeform(spawnPos, folder, targetPlayerName)
    if lifeformState.model and lifeformState.model.Parent then
        lifeformState.model:Destroy()
    end

    local targetFolder = folder or workspace:FindFirstChild("Backrooms_Level0") or workspace

    local model = Instance.new("Model", targetFolder)
    model.Name = "TheLifeform"

    local body = Instance.new("Part", model)
    body.Name = "Body"
    body.Size = Vector3.new(2, 5, 1)
    body.CFrame = CFrame.new(spawnPos)
    body.Anchored = true
    body.CanCollide = false
    body.Transparency = 1
    body.Material = Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic
    body.CastShadow = false
    body.Massless = true
    model.PrimaryPart = body

    local bb = Instance.new("BillboardGui", body)
    bb.Name = "LifeformImage"
    bb.Size = UDim2.new(0, 170, 0, 240)
    bb.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 2.5, 0)
    bb.AlwaysOnTop = false
    bb.LightInfluence = 0
    bb.MaxDistance = 250

    local img = Instance.new("ImageLabel", bb)
    img.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
    img.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    img.Image = LIFEFORM_IMG
    img.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Fit

    local snd = Instance.new("Sound", body)
    snd.Name = "ChaseSound"
    snd.SoundId = LIFEFORM_SOUND
    snd.Looped = true
    snd.Volume = 4
    snd.RollOffMaxDistance = 200
    snd.RollOffMode = Enum.RollOffMode.InverseTapered

    local light = Instance.new("PointLight", body)
    light.Color = Color3.fromRGB(180, 0, 0)
    light.Brightness = 3
    light.Range = 30
    light.Shadows = true

    lifeformState.model = model
    lifeformState.active = true

    task.spawn(function()
        local path = PathfindingService:CreatePath({
            AgentRadius = 2,
            AgentHeight = 5,
            AgentCanJump = false,
            AgentMaxSlope = 45,
        })

        local waypoints = {}
        local wpi = 1
        local cooldown = 0
        local SPEED = 22
        local DETECT_RANGE = 500

        while lifeformState.active and model.Parent do
            local targetPlr = Players:FindFirstChild(targetPlayerName) or LocalPlayer
            local chr = targetPlr and targetPlr.Character
            local th = chr and chr:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            local thum = chr and chr:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

            if th and thum and thum.Health > 0 then
                local myPos = body.Position
                local targetPos = th.Position
                local dist = (targetPos - myPos).Magnitude

                if dist <= DETECT_RANGE then
                    if not snd.IsPlaying then
                        pcall(function() snd:Play() end)
                    end

                    if tick() - cooldown > 0.4 then
                        cooldown = tick()
                        pcall(function() path:ComputeAsync(myPos, targetPos) end)
                        if path.Status == Enum.PathStatus.Success then
                            waypoints = path:GetWaypoints()
                            wpi = 2
                        end
                    end

                    local moveTarget
                    if wpi <= #waypoints then
                        local wp = waypoints[wpi]
                        moveTarget = wp.Position
                        if (Vector3.new(myPos.X, 0, myPos.Z) - Vector3.new(wp.Position.X, 0, wp.Position.Z)).Magnitude < 3 then
                            wpi = wpi + 1
                        end
                    else
                        moveTarget = targetPos
                    end

                    local dir = moveTarget - myPos
                    dir = Vector3.new(dir.X, 0, dir.Z)
                    if dir.Magnitude > 0.5 then
                        dir = dir.Unit
                        local newPos = myPos + dir * SPEED * 0.05
                        newPos = Vector3.new(newPos.X, myPos.Y, newPos.Z)
                        body.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(newPos, Vector3.new(targetPos.X, newPos.Y, targetPos.Z))
                    end

                    if dist < 4 then
                        thum.Health = 0
                        task.wait(2)
                    end
                else
                    if snd.IsPlaying then
                        pcall(function() snd:Stop() end)
                    end
                end
            else
                if snd.IsPlaying then
                    pcall(function() snd:Stop() end)
                end
            end

            task.wait(0.05)
        end
    end)

    notify("The Lifeform", "Ele acordou perseguindo " .. targetPlayerName .. ".")
    return model
end

local function clearLifeform()
    lifeformState.active = false
    if lifeformState.model then
        pcall(function() lifeformState.model:Destroy() end)
        lifeformState.model = nil
    end
end

-- ================== BACKROOMS LEVEL 0 ==================
local backroomsSaved = nil
local backroomsToken = 0

local function salvarLighting()
    if backroomsSaved then return end
    backroomsSaved = {
        Ambient = Lighting.Ambient,
        OutdoorAmbient = Lighting.OutdoorAmbient,
        Brightness = Lighting.Brightness,
        FogStart = Lighting.FogStart,
        FogEnd = Lighting.FogEnd,
        FogColor = Lighting.FogColor,
        GlobalShadows = Lighting.GlobalShadows,
        ExposureCompensation = Lighting.ExposureCompensation,
        EnvironmentDiffuseScale = Lighting.EnvironmentDiffuseScale,
        EnvironmentSpecularScale = Lighting.EnvironmentSpecularScale,
    }
end

local function restaurarLighting()
    if not backroomsSaved then return end
    for k, v in pairs(backroomsSaved) do
        pcall(function() Lighting[k] = v end)
    end
    backroomsSaved = nil
end

local function limparEfeitosLighting()
    for _, n in ipairs({ "Backrooms_CC", "Backrooms_Bloom", "BackroomsAtmosphere" }) do
        local o = Lighting:FindFirstChild(n)
        if o then o:Destroy() end
    end
    local a = workspace:FindFirstChild("BackroomsAtmosphere")
    if a then a:Destroy() end
end

local imageCache = {}
local function resolveImage(id)
    local clean = tostring(id):match("%d+")
    if not clean then return tostring(id) end
    if imageCache[clean] then return imageCache[clean] end

    local fallback = "rbxassetid://" .. clean
    local result = fallback
    local ok, objs = pcall(function() return game:GetObjects(fallback) end)
    if ok and objs and objs[1] then
        local o = objs[1]
        local tex
        if o:IsA("Decal") or o:IsA("Texture") then
            tex = o.Texture
        elseif o:IsA("ImageLabel") or o:IsA("ImageButton") then
            tex = o.Image
        else
            local d = o:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Decal", true) or o:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Texture", true)
            if d then tex = d.Texture end
        end
        if tex and tex ~= "" then result = tex end
        pcall(function() o:Destroy() end)
    end

    imageCache[clean] = result
    return result
end

local function spawnBackrooms(targetPartnerName)
    notify("Backrooms", "Gerando Level 0...")

    backroomsToken += 1
    local myToken = backroomsToken

    local old = workspace:FindFirstChild("Backrooms_Level0")
    if old then old:Destroy() end
    limparEfeitosLighting()
    clearLifeform()

    local folder = Instance.new("Folder")
    folder.Name = "Backrooms_Level0"
    folder.Parent = workspace

    local GRID = 24
    local CELL = 16
    local WALL_H = 14
    local T = 1.2
    local BASE = Vector3.new(0, 200, 0)
    local LIGHT_EVERY = 3

    local ROOM_SIZE = GRID * CELL
    local half = ROOM_SIZE / 2
    local floorY = BASE.Y
    local ceilY = floorY + WALL_H
    local ox, oz = BASE.X - half, BASE.Z - half

    local WALL_COLOR = Color3.fromRGB(238, 208, 86)
    local TRIM_COLOR = Color3.fromRGB(238, 226, 190)
    local WOOD_COLOR = Color3.fromRGB(96, 62, 36)
    local CARPET_COLOR = Color3.fromRGB(168, 130, 58)
    local CEIL_COLOR = Color3.fromRGB(232, 226, 190)
    local PANEL_COLOR = Color3.fromRGB(255, 252, 228)

    notify("Backrooms", "Carregando texturas...")
    local resolved, pending = {}, 0
    local function resolveAsync(key, id)
        pending += 1
        task.spawn(function()
            pcall(function() resolved[key] = resolveImage(id) end)
            pending -= 1
        end)
    end
    resolveAsync("wall", TEX_WALL)
    resolveAsync("floor", TEX_FLOOR)
    resolveAsync("ceil", TEX_CEILING)
    for i, id in ipairs(DRAWING_IDS) do resolveAsync("d" .. i, id) end

    local t0 = os.clock()
    while pending > 0 and os.clock() - t0 < 8 do task.wait(0.1) end
    if backroomsToken ~= myToken then error("backrooms cancelada") end

    local texWall = resolved.wall or TEX_WALL
    local texFloor = resolved.floor or TEX_FLOOR
    local texCeil = resolved.ceil or TEX_CEILING
    local drawImgs = {}
    for i, id in ipairs(DRAWING_IDS) do
        drawImgs[i] = resolved["d" .. i] or getRealImageIdDelta(id)
    end

    local built = 0
    local function mk(size, cf, color, mat, collide)
        local p = Instance.new("Part")
        p.Anchored = true
        p.Size = size
        p.CFrame = cf
        p.Color = color
        p.Material = mat or Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic
        p.CanCollide = collide and true or false
        p.CanQuery = collide and true or false
        p.CanTouch = false
        p.CastShadow = collide and true or false
        p.TopSurface = Enum.SurfaceType.Smooth
        p.BottomSurface = Enum.SurfaceType.Smooth
        p.Parent = folder
        built += 1
        if built % 150 == 0 then
            task.wait()
            if backroomsToken ~= myToken then error("backrooms cancelada") end
        end
        return p
    end

    local function addTextures(part, faces, tile, texture)
        pcall(function()
            for _, f in ipairs(faces) do
                local tex = Instance.new("Texture")
                tex.Texture = texture
                tex.Face = f
                tex.StudsPerTileU = tile
                tex.StudsPerTileV = tile
                tex.Parent = part
            end
        end)
    end

    local floor = mk(Vector3.new(ROOM_SIZE + T * 2, 2, ROOM_SIZE + T * 2), CFrame.new(BASE.X, floorY - 1, BASE.Z), CARPET_COLOR, Enum.Material.Fabric, true)
    floor.Name = "Floor"
    addTextures(floor, { Enum.NormalId.Top }, 5, texFloor)

    local ceil = mk(Vector3.new(ROOM_SIZE + T * 2, 2, ROOM_SIZE + T * 2), CFrame.new(BASE.X, ceilY + 1, BASE.Z), CEIL_COLOR, Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic, true)
    ceil.Name = "Ceiling"
    addTextures(ceil, { Enum.NormalId.Bottom }, 5, texCeil)

    local hW, vW = {}, {}
    for y = 0, GRID do
        hW[y] = {}
        for x = 1, GRID do hW[y][x] = true end
    end
    for y = 1, GRID do
        vW[y] = {}
        for x = 0, GRID do vW[y][x] = true end
    end

    local visited = {}
    for y = 1, GRID do visited[y] = {} end
    local stack = { { 1, 1 } }
    visited[1][1] = true
    while #stack > 0 do
        local cur = stack[#stack]
        local cx, cy = cur[1], cur[2]
        local nbs = {}
        if cx > 1 and not visited[cy][cx - 1] then table.insert(nbs, { cx - 1, cy }) end
        if cx < GRID and not visited[cy][cx + 1] then table.insert(nbs, { cx + 1, cy }) end
        if cy > 1 and not visited[cy - 1][cx] then table.insert(nbs, { cx, cy - 1 }) end
        if cy < GRID and not visited[cy + 1][cx] then table.insert(nbs, { cx, cy + 1 }) end
        if #nbs == 0 then
            table.remove(stack)
        else
            local n = nbs[math.random(#nbs)]
            local nx, ny = n[1], n[2]
            if nx == cx + 1 then vW[cy][cx] = false
            elseif nx == cx - 1 then vW[cy][cx - 1] = false
            elseif ny == cy + 1 then hW[cy][cx] = false
            else hW[cy - 1][cx] = false end
            visited[ny][nx] = true
            table.insert(stack, { nx, ny })
        end
    end

    for y = 1, GRID - 1 do
        for x = 1, GRID do
            if hW[y][x] and math.random() < 0.22 then hW[y][x] = false end
        end
    end
    for y = 1, GRID do
        for x = 1, GRID - 1 do
            if vW[y][x] and math.random() < 0.22 then vW[y][x] = false end
        end
    end

    local pillarSpots = {}
    local function abrirSala(rx, ry, rw, rh, comPilares)
        for y = ry, ry + rh - 1 do
            for x = rx, rx + rw - 2 do vW[y][x] = false end
        end
        for y = ry, ry + rh - 2 do
            for x = rx, rx + rw - 1 do hW[y][x] = false end
        end
        if comPilares then
            for yi = ry, ry + rh - 2 do
                for xi = rx, rx + rw - 2 do
                    if (xi - rx) % 2 == 0 and (yi - ry) % 2 == 0 and math.random() > 0.15 then
                        table.insert(pillarSpots, { xi, yi })
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end

    for _ = 1, 9 do
        local rw, rh = math.random(3, 6), math.random(3, 6)
        local rx = math.random(1, GRID - rw + 1)
        local ry = math.random(1, GRID - rh + 1)
        abrirSala(rx, ry, rw, rh, true)
    end

    local scx = math.floor(GRID / 2)
    abrirSala(scx - 1, scx - 1, 3, 3, false)
    local startPos = Vector3.new(ox + (scx - 0.5) * CELL, floorY + 5, oz + (scx - 0.5) * CELL)

    local runs = {}
    local function wallRun(isH, cx, cz, length)
        local size = isH and Vector3.new(length + T, WALL_H, T) or Vector3.new(T, WALL_H, length + T)
        local wall = mk(size, CFrame.new(cx, floorY + WALL_H / 2, cz), WALL_COLOR, Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic, true)
        wall.Name = "Wall"
        addTextures(wall, isH and { Enum.NormalId.Front, Enum.NormalId.Back } or { Enum.NormalId.Left, Enum.NormalId.Right }, 5, texWall)

        local railOn = math.random() < 0.3
        local lenV = length + T
        for _, s in ipairs({ -1, 1 }) do
            local nrm = isH and Vector3.new(0, 0, s) or Vector3.new(s, 0, 0)
            local function strip(h, thick, y, col)
                local sz = isH and Vector3.new(lenV, h, thick) or Vector3.new(thick, h, lenV)
                return mk(sz, CFrame.new(cx, y, cz) + nrm * (T / 2 + thick / 2), col, Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic, false)
            end
            strip(0.9, 0.3, floorY + 0.45, TRIM_COLOR)
            strip(0.5, 0.25, floorY + WALL_H - 0.25, TRIM_COLOR)
            if railOn then strip(0.25, 0.4, floorY + 1.7, WOOD_COLOR) end
        end

        table.insert(runs, { isH = isH, cx = cx, cz = cz, length = length })
    end

    for y = 0, GRID do
        local x = 1
        while x <= GRID do
            if hW[y][x] then
                local s = x
                while x <= GRID and hW[y][x] do x += 1 end
                local len = (x - s) * CELL
                wallRun(true, ox + (s - 1) * CELL + len / 2, oz + y * CELL, len)
            else
                x += 1
            end
        end
    end
    for x = 0, GRID do
        local y = 1
        while y <= GRID do
            if vW[y][x] then
                local s = y
                while y <= GRID and vW[y][x] do y += 1 end
                local len = (y - s) * CELL
                wallRun(false, ox + x * CELL, oz + (s - 1) * CELL + len / 2, len)
            else
                y += 1
            end
        end
    end

    for _, sp in ipairs(pillarSpots) do
        if not (math.abs(sp[1] - scx) <= 3 and math.abs(sp[2] - scx) <= 3) then
            local px, pz = ox + sp[1] * CELL, oz + sp[2] * CELL
            local pil = mk(Vector3.new(5, WALL_H, 5), CFrame.new(px, floorY + WALL_H / 2, pz), WALL_COLOR, Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic, true)
            pil.Name = "Pillar"
            addTextures(pil, { Enum.NormalId.Front, Enum.NormalId.Back, Enum.NormalId.Left, Enum.NormalId.Right }, 5, texWall)
            mk(Vector3.new(5.6, 0.9, 5.6), CFrame.new(px, floorY + 0.45, pz), TRIM_COLOR, Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic, false)
            mk(Vector3.new(5.5, 0.5, 5.5), CFrame.new(px, floorY + WALL_H - 0.25, pz), TRIM_COLOR, Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic, false)
        end
    end

    local panels = {}
    for y = 1, GRID do
        for x = 1, GRID do
            local px, pz = ox + (x - 0.5) * CELL, oz + (y - 0.5) * CELL
            local panel = mk(Vector3.new(7, 0.25, 3.4), CFrame.new(px, ceilY - 0.13, pz), PANEL_COLOR, Enum.Material.Neon, false)
            panel.Name = "Lamp"
            local light
            if x % LIGHT_EVERY == 0 and y % LIGHT_EVERY == 0 then
                light = Instance.new("SurfaceLight")
                light.Face = Enum.NormalId.Bottom
                light.Brightness = 2.6
                light.Range = 42
                light.Angle = 130
                light.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 243, 200)
                light.Shadows = false
                light.Parent = panel
            end
            panels[#panels + 1] = { part = panel, light = light }
        end
    end

    task.spawn(function()
        while backroomsToken == myToken and folder.Parent do
            task.wait(math.random(15, 50) / 10)
            local item = panels[math.random(#panels)]
            for _ = 1, math.random(3, 8) do
                if backroomsToken ~= myToken or not item.part.Parent then break end
                local on = math.random() > 0.45
                item.part.Material = on and Enum.Material.Neon or Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic
                item.part.Color = on and PANEL_COLOR or Color3.fromRGB(150, 148, 125)
                if item.light then item.light.Enabled = on end
                task.wait(0.05 + math.random() * 0.08)
            end
            if item.part.Parent then
                item.part.Material = Enum.Material.Neon
                item.part.Color = PANEL_COLOR
                if item.light then item.light.Enabled = true end
            end
        end
    end)

    for _ = 1, 70 do
        local d = math.random(4, 14)
        local px = ox + 10 + math.random() * (ROOM_SIZE - 20)
        local pz = oz + 10 + math.random() * (ROOM_SIZE - 20)
        local st = mk(Vector3.new(0.05, d, d * (0.7 + math.random() * 0.5)),
            CFrame.new(px, floorY + 0.03, pz) * CFrame.Angles(0, math.rad(math.random(360)), 0) * CFrame.Angles(0, 0, math.rad(90)),
            Color3.fromRGB(88, 66, 28), Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic, false)
        st.Shape = Enum.PartType.Cylinder
        st.Transparency = 0.5 + math.random() * 0.15
        st.Name = "Stain"
    end

    local function wallPoint(run, dist)
        local s = math.random() < 0.5 and -1 or 1
        local nrm = run.isH and Vector3.new(0, 0, s) or Vector3.new(s, 0, 0)
        local along = (math.random() - 0.5) * math.max(run.length - 8, 0)
        local base = run.isH and Vector3.new(run.cx + along, 0, run.cz) or Vector3.new(run.cx, 0, run.cz + along)
        local pos = base + nrm * (T / 2 + dist)
        return Vector3.new(pos.X, floorY, pos.Z), nrm
    end

    local drawIdx = 0
    for _ = 1, 32 do
        local run = runs[math.random(#runs)]
        if run.length >= 10 and #DRAWING_IDS > 0 then
            local pos, nrm = wallPoint(run, 0.04)
            drawIdx = drawIdx % #DRAWING_IDS + 1
            local canvas = mk(Vector3.new(9, 9, 0.05), CFrame.lookAt(pos + Vector3.new(0, 6.5, 0), pos + Vector3.new(0, 6.5, 0) + nrm), WALL_COLOR, Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic, false)
            canvas.Name = "WallDrawing"
            canvas.Transparency = 1
            local sg = Instance.new("SurfaceGui")
            sg.Face = Enum.NormalId.Front
            sg.AlwaysOnTop = false
            sg.LightInfluence = 1
            sg.Parent = canvas
            local img = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
            img.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
            img.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            img.Image = drawImgs[drawIdx]
            img.ImageTransparency = 0.08
            img.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Fit
            img.Parent = sg
        end
    end

    for _ = 1, 35 do
        local run = runs[math.random(#runs)]
        if run.length >= 8 then
            local pos, nrm = wallPoint(run, 0.07)
            local cf = CFrame.lookAt(pos + Vector3.new(0, 1.6, 0), pos + Vector3.new(0, 1.6, 0) + nrm)
            mk(Vector3.new(0.9, 1.3, 0.12), cf, TRIM_COLOR, Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic, false)
            mk(Vector3.new(0.18, 0.3, 0.05), cf * CFrame.new(-0.2, 0.2, -0.08), Color3.fromRGB(40, 35, 25), Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic, false)
            mk(Vector3.new(0.18, 0.3, 0.05), cf * CFrame.new(0.2, 0.2, -0.08), Color3.fromRGB(40, 35, 25), Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic, false)
            mk(Vector3.new(0.18, 0.3, 0.05), cf * CFrame.new(-0.2, -0.2, -0.08), Color3.fromRGB(40, 35, 25), Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic, false)
            mk(Vector3.new(0.18, 0.3, 0.05), cf * CFrame.new(0.2, -0.2, -0.08), Color3.fromRGB(40, 35, 25), Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic, false)
        end
    end

    for _ = 1, 12 do
        pcall(function()
            local run = runs[math.random(#runs)]
            if run.length < 12 then return end
            local pos, nrm = wallPoint(run, 1.3)
            local center = pos + Vector3.new(0, 3.1, 0)
            local cf = CFrame.lookAt(center, center + nrm)
            local body = mk(Vector3.new(2.6, 6.2, 2.2), cf, Color3.fromRGB(112, 58, 38), Enum.Material.Metal, true)
            body.Name = "Locker"
            for i = 0, 2 do
                mk(Vector3.new(1.6, 0.14, 0.06), cf * CFrame.new(0, 1.4 + i * 0.3, -1.13), Color3.fromRGB(30, 20, 15), Enum.Material.Metal, false)
            end
        end)
    end

    for _ = 1, 10 do
        pcall(function()
            local run = runs[math.random(#runs)]
            if run.length < 10 then return end
            local pos = wallPoint(run, 1.6)
            local dark = Color3.fromRGB(45, 38, 30)
            mk(Vector3.new(1.4, 0.2, 1.4), CFrame.new(pos + Vector3.new(0, 0.1, 0)), dark, Enum.Material.Metal, false)
            mk(Vector3.new(0.2, 5.2, 0.2), CFrame.new(pos + Vector3.new(0, 2.7, 0)), dark, Enum.Material.Metal, false)
            local shade = mk(Vector3.new(1.8, 2.2, 2.2), CFrame.new(pos + Vector3.new(0, 5.6, 0)) * CFrame.Angles(0, 0, math.rad(90)), Color3.fromRGB(240, 200, 100), Enum.Material.Neon, false)
            shade.Shape = Enum.PartType.Cylinder
            shade.Transparency = 0.15
            local pl = Instance.new("PointLight")
            pl.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 214, 120)
            pl.Brightness = 1.4
            pl.Range = 16
            pl.Shadows = false
            pl.Parent = shade
        end)
    end

    for _ = 1, 8 do
        pcall(function()
            local run = runs[math.random(#runs)]
            if run.length < 10 then return end
            local pos, nrm = wallPoint(run, 2.8)
            local center = pos + Vector3.new(0, 2.6, 0)
            local cf = CFrame.lookAt(center, center + nrm)
            local wood = Color3.fromRGB(110, 70, 40)
            mk(Vector3.new(5, 0.3, 2.4), cf, wood, Enum.Material.Wood, true)
            for _, sx in ipairs({ -1, 1 }) do
                for _, sz in ipairs({ -1, 1 }) do
                    mk(Vector3.new(0.3, 2.5, 0.3), cf * CFrame.new(sx * 2.2, -1.3, sz * 1.0), wood, Enum.Material.Wood, true)
                end
            end
        end)
    end

    salvarLighting()
    pcall(function()
        Lighting.Ambient = Color3.fromRGB(125, 108, 58)
        Lighting.OutdoorAmbient = Color3.fromRGB(110, 95, 50)
        Lighting.Brightness = 2
        Lighting.GlobalShadows = true
        Lighting.ExposureCompensation = 0.15
        Lighting.EnvironmentDiffuseScale = 0
        Lighting.EnvironmentSpecularScale = 0
        Lighting.FogColor = Color3.fromRGB(70, 58, 25)
        Lighting.FogStart = 60
        Lighting.FogEnd = 650
    end)

    pcall(function()
        local cc = Instance.new("ColorCorrectionEffect")
        cc.Name = "Backrooms_CC"
        cc.TintColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 243, 175)
        cc.Saturation = 0.08
        cc.Contrast = 0.08
        cc.Brightness = -0.01
        cc.Parent = Lighting

        local bl = Instance.new("BloomEffect")
        bl.Name = "Backrooms_Bloom"
        bl.Intensity = 0.35
        bl.Size = 22
        bl.Threshold = 0.92
        bl.Parent = Lighting

        local atm = Instance.new("Atmosphere")
        atm.Name = "BackroomsAtmosphere"
        atm.Density = 0.28
        atm.Offset = 0
        atm.Color = Color3.fromRGB(200, 175, 95)
        atm.Decay = Color3.fromRGB(70, 55, 20)
        atm.Glare = 0
        atm.Haze = 0
        atm.Parent = Lighting
    end)

    pcall(function()
        local sndPart = mk(Vector3.new(1, 1, 1), CFrame.new(startPos), Color3.new(0, 0, 0), Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic, false)
        sndPart.Name = "BackroomsMusicEmitter"
        sndPart.Transparency = 1

        local sound = Instance.new("Sound")
        sound.SoundId = BACKROOMS_MUSIC
        sound.Looped = true
        sound.Volume = 2.5
        sound.RollOffMaxDistance = 10000
        sound.Parent = sndPart
        sound:Play()
    end)

    task.spawn(function()
        task.wait(1)
        local targetCFrame = CFrame.new(startPos)
        local chr = LocalPlayer.Character
        local hrp = chr and chr:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if hrp then
            hrp.Anchored = true
            hrp.CFrame = targetCFrame
            task.wait(0.3)
            hrp.Anchored = false
        end

        if targetPartnerName and targetPartnerName ~= "" then
            local partner = Players:FindFirstChild(targetPartnerName)
            if partner and partner.Character and partner.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
                partner.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = targetCFrame + Vector3.new(3, 0, 3)
            end
        end

        notify("Backrooms", "Level 0 pronto!")
    end)

    return true
end

local function clearBackrooms()
    backroomsToken += 1
    clearLifeform()
    local f = workspace:FindFirstChild("Backrooms_Level0")
    if f then f:Destroy() end
    limparEfeitosLighting()
    restaurarLighting()
    notify("Backrooms", "Backrooms removidas.")
    return true
end

-- ================== POOLROOMS (LEVEL 37 - SALÃO INFINITO) ==================
-- Salão infinito: piso azulejado coberto por água rasa turquesa, colunas e paredes brancas de azulejo,
-- teto branco com painéis fluorescentes frios em grade perfeita e névoa leve ao fundo.
-- O mundo é feito de "chunks" 64x64 gerados em volta de você (nunca acaba).
-- Sombras: usa Future (se o executor tiver sethiddenproperty) + luzes COM sombra nos painéis mais próximos.
local POOL = { token = 0, saved = nil, S = nil, hidden = {} }

do
    local TweenService = game:GetService("TweenService")
    local Debris       = game:GetService("Debris")

    local CH           = 64                       -- tamanho do chunk
    local OFFS         = { -24, -8, 8, 24 }       -- posição das colunas dentro do chunk (grade de 16 studs)
    local PANEL_OFFS   = { -32, -16, 0, 16 }      -- painéis de luz: centro de cada vão entre 4 colunas
    local COL_W        = 3.6                      -- largura das colunas
    local WALL_H       = 24                       -- pé-direito
    local POOL_Y       = 1000                     -- topo do piso
    local WATER_H      = 1.6                      -- água rasa (dá pra andar)
    local RADIUS       = 2                        -- chunks carregados em volta de você
    local TILE         = 4                        -- studs por azulejo
    local LIGHT_COUNT  = 36                       -- quantos painéis têm luz real (os mais próximos)
    local SHADOW_COUNT = 10                       -- quantos desses projetam sombra
    local WALL_TEX     = "rbxassetid://122349893272800"

    local C_TILE  = Color3.fromRGB(246, 248, 248)
    local C_FLOOR = Color3.fromRGB(222, 238, 236)
    local C_WATER = Color3.fromRGB(70, 205, 198)
    local C_WET   = Color3.fromRGB(60, 190, 185)
    local C_TRIM  = Color3.fromRGB(170, 178, 180)
    local C_CEIL  = Color3.fromRGB(244, 246, 248)
    local C_FRAME = Color3.fromRGB(150, 155, 160)
    local C_PANEL = Color3.fromRGB(232, 244, 255)
    local C_LIGHT = Color3.fromRGB(214, 232, 255)

    local FACES_FB    = { Enum.NormalId.Front, Enum.NormalId.Back }
    local FACES_LR    = { Enum.NormalId.Left, Enum.NormalId.Right }
    local FACES_TOP   = { Enum.NormalId.Top }
    local FACES_SIDES = { Enum.NormalId.Front, Enum.NormalId.Back, Enum.NormalId.Left, Enum.NormalId.Right }

    ----------------------------------------------------------------
    -- PRIMITIVAS
    ----------------------------------------------------------------
    local function mkPart(parent, size, cf, color, mat, collide)
        local p = Instance.new("Part")
        p.Anchored = true
        p.Size = size
        p.CFrame = cf
        p.Color = color
        p.Material = mat or Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic
        p.CanCollide = collide and true or false
        p.CanQuery = collide and true or false
        p.CanTouch = false
        p.TopSurface = Enum.SurfaceType.Smooth
        p.BottomSurface = Enum.SurfaceType.Smooth
        p.Parent = parent
        return p
    end

    -- laje azulejada brilhante (textura nas faces pedidas)
    local function slab(S, parent, size, cf, faces, color, collide)
        local p = mkPart(parent, size, cf, color or C_TILE, Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic, collide ~= false)
        p.Reflectance = 0.12
        for _, f in ipairs(faces or FACES_FB) do
            local t = Instance.new("Texture")
            t.Texture = S.tex
            t.Face = f
            t.StudsPerTileU = TILE
            t.StudsPerTileV = TILE
            t.Parent = p
        end
        S.count += 1
        if S.count % 70 == 0 then task.wait() end
        return p
    end

    -- faixa úmida na base (desgaste/umidade perto da água)
    local function wetBand(parent, w, d, x, z)
        local b = mkPart(parent, Vector3.new(w, 3, d), CFrame.new(x, POOL_Y + 1.5, z), C_WET, Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic, false)
        b.Transparency = 0.6
        b.Reflectance = 0.1
        b.CastShadow = false
        return b
    end

    ----------------------------------------------------------------
    -- CHUNK
    ----------------------------------------------------------------
    local function buildChunk(S, cx, cz)
        if POOL.token ~= S.token then return end
        local key = cx .. "," .. cz
        if S.chunks[key] then return end

        local folder = Instance.new("Folder")
        folder.Name = "Chunk_" .. key
        folder.Parent = S.folder
        local chunk = { folder = folder, cx = cx, cz = cz, panels = {} }
        S.chunks[key] = chunk

        local rng = Random.new(cx * 7349 + cz * 9157 + 11)
        local wx, wz = cx * CH, cz * CH
        local ceilBottom = POOL_Y + WALL_H

        local ok, err = pcall(function()
            -- piso azulejado
            slab(S, folder, Vector3.new(CH, 2, CH), CFrame.new(wx, POOL_Y - 1, wz), FACES_TOP, C_FLOOR)

            -- água rasa turquesa (reflexiva, calma)
            local w = mkPart(folder, Vector3.new(CH, WATER_H, CH), CFrame.new(wx, POOL_Y + WATER_H / 2, wz), C_WATER, Enum.Material.Glass, false)
            w.Name = "PoolWater"
            w.Transparency = 0.55
            w.Reflectance = 0.22
            w.CastShadow = false

            -- teto branco
            local ceil = mkPart(folder, Vector3.new(CH, 2, CH), CFrame.new(wx, ceilBottom + 1, wz), C_CEIL, Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic, true)
            ceil.Name = "Ceiling"
            ceil.Reflectance = 0.04

            -- colunas brancas em grade simétrica
            for _, dx in ipairs(OFFS) do
                for _, dz in ipairs(OFFS) do
                    local x, z = wx + dx, wz + dz
                    slab(S, folder, Vector3.new(COL_W, WALL_H, COL_W), CFrame.new(x, POOL_Y + WALL_H / 2, z), FACES_SIDES, C_TILE)
                    wetBand(folder, COL_W + 0.14, COL_W + 0.14, x, z)
                    local cap = mkPart(folder, Vector3.new(COL_W + 0.9, 0.8, COL_W + 0.9), CFrame.new(x, ceilBottom - 0.4, z), C_TRIM, Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic, false)
                    cap.Reflectance = 0.05
                end
            end

            -- paredes de azulejo (divisórias ocasionais entre colunas)
            local gap = (OFFS[2] - OFFS[1]) - COL_W
            for i = 1, #OFFS - 1 do
                for j = 1, #OFFS do
                    if rng:NextNumber() < 0.16 then
                        local x, z = wx + (OFFS[i] + OFFS[i + 1]) / 2, wz + OFFS[j]
                        slab(S, folder, Vector3.new(gap, WALL_H, 1.2), CFrame.new(x, POOL_Y + WALL_H / 2, z), FACES_FB, C_TILE)
                        wetBand(folder, gap + 0.02, 1.34, x, z)
                    end
                end
            end
            for i = 1, #OFFS do
                for j = 1, #OFFS - 1 do
                    if rng:NextNumber() < 0.16 then
                        local x, z = wx + OFFS[i], wz + (OFFS[j] + OFFS[j + 1]) / 2
                        slab(S, folder, Vector3.new(1.2, WALL_H, gap), CFrame.new(x, POOL_Y + WALL_H / 2, z), FACES_LR, C_TILE)
                        wetBand(folder, 1.34, gap + 0.02, x, z)
                    end
                end
            end

            -- painéis fluorescentes frios em grade perfeita
            for _, u in ipairs(PANEL_OFFS) do
                for _, v in ipairs(PANEL_OFFS) do
                    local x, z = wx + u, wz + v
                    local frame = mkPart(folder, Vector3.new(10.4, 0.5, 4.4), CFrame.new(x, ceilBottom - 0.25, z), C_FRAME, Enum.Material.Metal, false)
                    frame.CastShadow = false
                    local pan = mkPart(folder, Vector3.new(9.6, 0.3, 3.6), CFrame.new(x, ceilBottom - 0.55, z), C_PANEL, Enum.Material.Neon, false)
                    pan.Name = "LightPanel"
                    pan.CastShadow = false
                    table.insert(chunk.panels, { part = pan, light = nil, d = 0 })
                end
            end
        end)
        if not ok then warn("[Poolrooms] chunk " .. key .. ": " .. tostring(err)) end
    end

    local function unloadChunk(S, key)
        local ch = S.chunks[key]
        if not ch then return end
        S.chunks[key] = nil
        pcall(function() ch.folder:Destroy() end)
    end

    local function streamStep(S, pcx, pcz, radius)
        for ring = 0, radius do
            for dx = -ring, ring do
                for dz = -ring, ring do
                    if POOL.token ~= S.token then return end
                    if math.max(math.abs(dx), math.abs(dz)) == ring then
                        buildChunk(S, pcx + dx, pcz + dz)
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        for key, ch in pairs(S.chunks) do
            if math.max(math.abs(ch.cx - pcx), math.abs(ch.cz - pcz)) > RADIUS + 1 then
                unloadChunk(S, key)
            end
        end
    end

    ----------------------------------------------------------------
    -- LUZES (só os painéis mais próximos têm luz real e sombra)
    ----------------------------------------------------------------
    local function updateLights(S, pos)
        local list = {}
        for _, ch in pairs(S.chunks) do
            for _, pn in ipairs(ch.panels) do
                if pn.part.Parent then
                    pn.d = (pn.part.Position - pos).Magnitude
                    list[#list + 1] = pn
                end
            end
        end
        table.sort(list, function(a, b) return a.d < b.d end)
        for i, pn in ipairs(list) do
            if i <= LIGHT_COUNT then
                if not (pn.light and pn.light.Parent) then
                    local l = Instance.new("SurfaceLight")
                    l.Face = Enum.NormalId.Bottom
                    l.Angle = 170
                    l.Range = 58
                    l.Brightness = 1.7
                    l.Color = C_LIGHT
                    l.Shadows = false
                    l.Parent = pn.part
                    pn.light = l
                end
                pn.light.Shadows = i <= SHADOW_COUNT
            elseif pn.light then
                pn.light:Destroy()
                pn.light = nil
            end
        end
    end

    ----------------------------------------------------------------
    -- AMBIENTE (luz fria, sombra, névoa)
    ----------------------------------------------------------------
    local LIGHT_PROPS = {
        "Ambient", "OutdoorAmbient", "Brightness", "ColorShift_Top", "ColorShift_Bottom",
        "ExposureCompensation", "ClockTime", "FogColor", "FogStart", "FogEnd", "GlobalShadows",
        "ShadowSoftness", "EnvironmentDiffuseScale", "EnvironmentSpecularScale",
    }

    local function envSave()
        local s = {}
        for _, k in ipairs(LIGHT_PROPS) do
            pcall(function() s[k] = Lighting[k] end)
        end
        pcall(function() s.Technology = gethiddenproperty(Lighting, "Technology") end)
        return s
    end

    local function envApply()
        local function set(k, v) pcall(function() Lighting[k] = v end) end
        set("ClockTime", 14)
        set("Brightness", 2.2)
        set("Ambient", Color3.fromRGB(125, 148, 156))
        set("OutdoorAmbient", Color3.fromRGB(140, 160, 168))
        set("ColorShift_Top", Color3.fromRGB(230, 245, 255))
        set("ColorShift_Bottom", Color3.fromRGB(90, 190, 185))
        set("ExposureCompensation", 0.25)
        set("GlobalShadows", true)
        set("ShadowSoftness", 0.35)
        set("EnvironmentDiffuseScale", 0.6)
        set("EnvironmentSpecularScale", 1)
        set("FogColor", Color3.fromRGB(205, 228, 236))
        set("FogStart", 70)
        set("FogEnd", 250)
        pcall(function() sethiddenproperty(Lighting, "Technology", Enum.Technology.Future) end)

        -- remove atmosferas do jogo (só uma funciona por vez)
        pcall(function()
            for _, c in ipairs(Lighting:GetChildren()) do
                if c:IsA("Atmosphere") then
                    table.insert(POOL.hidden, c)
                    c.Parent = nil
                end
            end
        end)

        pcall(function()
            local cc = Instance.new("ColorCorrectionEffect")
            cc.Name = "Pool_CC"
            cc.TintColor = Color3.fromRGB(235, 248, 255)
            cc.Saturation = 0.1
            cc.Contrast = 0.08
            cc.Brightness = 0.02
            cc.Parent = Lighting

            local bl = Instance.new("BloomEffect")
            bl.Name = "Pool_Bloom"
            bl.Intensity = 0.35
            bl.Size = 24
            bl.Threshold = 0.95
            bl.Parent = Lighting

            local at = Instance.new("Atmosphere")
            at.Name = "Pool_Atmosphere"
            at.Density = 0.22
            at.Offset = 0
            at.Color = Color3.fromRGB(210, 230, 238)
            at.Decay = Color3.fromRGB(150, 180, 190)
            at.Glare = 0
            at.Haze = 0.9
            at.Parent = Lighting
        end)
    end

    local function envRestore()
        local s = POOL.saved
        if not s then return end
        POOL.saved = nil
        for _, k in ipairs(LIGHT_PROPS) do
            if s[k] ~= nil then pcall(function() Lighting[k] = s[k] end) end
        end
        if s.Technology ~= nil then
            pcall(function() sethiddenproperty(Lighting, "Technology", s.Technology) end)
        end
    end

    ----------------------------------------------------------------
    -- API
    ----------------------------------------------------------------
    function POOL.clear(silent)
        POOL.token += 1
        local S = POOL.S
        POOL.S = nil
        if S then
            if S.conn then pcall(function() S.conn:Disconnect() end) end
            if S.folder then pcall(function() S.folder:Destroy() end) end
        end
        for _, n in ipairs({ "Pool_CC", "Pool_Bloom", "Pool_Rays", "Pool_Atmosphere" }) do
            local o = Lighting:FindFirstChild(n)
            if o then o:Destroy() end
        end
        for _, a in ipairs(POOL.hidden) do
            pcall(function() a.Parent = Lighting end)
        end
        POOL.hidden = {}
        envRestore()
        if S and not silent then notify("Poolrooms", "Poolrooms removidas.") end
        return true
    end

    function POOL.spawn(partnerName)
        notify("Poolrooms", "Gerando Poolrooms infinitas...")
        POOL.clear(true)
        if workspace:FindFirstChild("Backrooms_Level0") then pcall(clearBackrooms) end
        local myToken = POOL.token

        local S = { chunks = {}, count = 0, tex = WALL_TEX, token = myToken }
        POOL.S = S
        local folder = Instance.new("Folder")
        folder.Name = "Poolrooms_Level"
        folder.Parent = workspace
        S.folder = folder

        pcall(function() S.tex = resolveImage(WALL_TEX) end)
        if POOL.token ~= myToken then return end

        POOL.saved = envSave()
        envApply()

        -- primeiro só o entorno imediato (rápido), depois o resto vai sendo gerado
        streamStep(S, 0, 0, 1)
        if POOL.token ~= myToken then return end

        local spawnCF = CFrame.new(16, POOL_Y + 6, 16)
        local function teleportMe()
            local chr = LocalPlayer.Character
            local hrp = chr and chr:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if hrp then
                hrp.Anchored = true
                hrp.CFrame = spawnCF
                task.wait(0.3)
                hrp.Anchored = false
            end
        end
        teleportMe()
        if partnerName and partnerName ~= "" then
            local partner = Players:FindFirstChild(partnerName)
            if partner and partner.Character and partner.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
                partner.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = spawnCF + Vector3.new(3, 0, 3)
            end
        end

        -- se você morrer/respawnar, volta pro salão
        S.conn = LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(c)
            local h = c:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 10)
            if h and POOL.token == myToken then
                task.wait(0.5)
                h.CFrame = spawnCF
            end
        end)

        -- streaming infinito + gerenciador de luzes/sombras
        task.spawn(function()
            while POOL.token == myToken do
                local c = LocalPlayer.Character
                local h = c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if h then
                    local pcx = math.floor((h.Position.X + CH / 2) / CH)
                    local pcz = math.floor((h.Position.Z + CH / 2) / CH)
                    pcall(streamStep, S, pcx, pcz, RADIUS)
                    pcall(updateLights, S, h.Position)
                end
                task.wait(0.35)
            end
        end)

        -- ondulações suaves na água (gotas ao redor + passos)
        task.spawn(function()
            local fx = Instance.new("Folder")
            fx.Name = "Ripples"
            fx.Parent = folder
            local function ring(x, z, size, life)
                local r = Instance.new("Part")
                r.Shape = Enum.PartType.Cylinder
                r.Anchored, r.CanCollide, r.CanQuery, r.CanTouch, r.CastShadow = true, false, false, false, false
                r.Material = Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic
                r.Color = Color3.fromRGB(225, 250, 248)
                r.Transparency = 0.55
                r.Size = Vector3.new(0.05, 0.6, 0.6)
                r.CFrame = CFrame.new(x, POOL_Y + WATER_H + 0.04, z) * CFrame.Angles(0, 0, math.rad(90))
                r.Parent = fx
                TweenService:Create(r, TweenInfo.new(life, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {
                    Size = Vector3.new(0.05, size, size),
                    Transparency = 1,
                }):Play()
                Debris:AddItem(r, life + 0.1)
            end
            local lastDrop = 0
            while POOL.token == myToken do
                local c = LocalPlayer.Character
                local h = c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if h and h.Position.Y < POOL_Y + WATER_H + 5 then
                    local p = h.Position
                    local sp = Vector3.new(h.AssemblyLinearVelocity.X, 0, h.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Z).Magnitude
                    if sp > 4 then ring(p.X, p.Z, 4.5, 1.1) end
                    if os.clock() - lastDrop > 0.9 then
                        lastDrop = os.clock()
                        ring(p.X + math.random(-30, 30), p.Z + math.random(-30, 30), 5, 2.2)
                    end
                end
                task.wait(0.3)
            end
        end)

        notify("Poolrooms", "Pronto! Poolrooms infinitas.")
        return true
    end
end

-- ================== LOCUST (REANIMATE) + REDE ==================
-- Script "Locust" (FEVerse) embutido como função. Liga/desliga localmente ou pela rede:
--   ;LOCUST_ON <alvo|ALL>      ;LOCUST_OFF <alvo|ALL>
-- Controles: X = modo kill | Q = agachar | C = sentar | E = waypoint/teleporte | Shift = correr
local LOCUST = { running = false, busy = false, stopFn = nil, fem = nil, users = {}, marks = {} }

-- quem está com Locust ligado (todo cliente do hub fica sabendo pela rede)
function LOCUST.announce(on)
    local me = LocalPlayer.Name
    local was = LOCUST.users[me] == true
    if was == on then return end
    LOCUST.users[me] = on or nil
    sendNetCmd(";LOCUST_STATE " .. me .. " " .. (on and "ON" or "OFF"))
end

-- marca visual (contorno + etiqueta) no alvo que está com Locust, pra todo mundo do hub ver
function LOCUST.mark(name, on)
    local old = LOCUST.marks[name]
    if old then
        if old.conn then old.conn:Disconnect() end
        for _, o in ipairs(old.objs) do pcall(function() o:Destroy() end) end
        LOCUST.marks[name] = nil
    end
    if not on then return end
    local plr = Players:FindFirstChild(name)
    if not plr then return end
    local st = { objs = {} }
    LOCUST.marks[name] = st
    local function apply(char)
        for _, o in ipairs(st.objs) do pcall(function() o:Destroy() end) end
        st.objs = {}
        if not char then return end
        local hl = Instance.new("Highlight")
        hl.Name = "F1L_LocustHL"
        hl.FillColor, hl.OutlineColor = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0), Color3.fromRGB(255, 40, 40)
        hl.FillTransparency, hl.OutlineTransparency = 0.75, 0
        hl.DepthMode = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop
        hl.Parent = char
        table.insert(st.objs, hl)
        -- etiqueta presa num acessório (os acessórios seguem o corpo do Locust)
        task.spawn(function()
            local handle
            local t0 = os.clock()
            repeat
                for _, a in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
                    if a:IsA("Accoutrement") then
                        handle = a:FindFirstChild("Handle")
                        if handle then break end
                    end
                end
                if not handle then task.wait(0.3) end
            until handle or os.clock() - t0 > 6 or not char.Parent
            if not handle or LOCUST.marks[name] ~= st then return end
            local bb = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
            bb.Name = "F1L_LocustTag"
            bb.Size = UDim2.new(0, 150, 0, 28)
            bb.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 4, 0)
            bb.AlwaysOnTop = true
            bb.Adornee = handle
            bb.Parent = handle
            local lb = Instance.new("TextLabel", bb)
            lb.Size, lb.BackgroundTransparency = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0), 1
            lb.Text = "LOCUST"
            lb.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 60, 60)
            lb.TextStrokeTransparency = 0
            lb.Font, lb.TextSize = Enum.Font.GothamBlack, 18
            table.insert(st.objs, bb)
        end)
    end
    if plr.Character then task.spawn(apply, plr.Character) end
    st.conn = plr.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(c)
        task.wait(1.5)
        if LOCUST.marks[name] == st then apply(c) end
    end)
end

function LOCUST.setUser(name, on)
    if name == LocalPlayer.Name then
        LOCUST.users[name] = on or nil
        return
    end
    local was = LOCUST.users[name] == true
    LOCUST.users[name] = on or nil
    LOCUST.mark(name, on)
    if was ~= on then notify("Locust", name .. (on and " ligou o Locust." or " desligou o Locust.")) end
end

local LOCUST_ACCESSORIES = {
    105900284766105, 89011214394076, 80591929991021, 74841586768030, 79723296413410,
    71673828053110, 18626055142, 18625377030, 17314457081, 17606680159,
}

-- ACESSÓRIOS DO LOCUST
-- Acessório colocado só pelo cliente (GetObjects + AddAccessory) é LOCAL: só você vê e some quando o servidor
-- troca o personagem (respawn). Pra TODO MUNDO ver, o servidor precisa registrar o acessório. Por isso:
--   1) tenta vestir pelo remote de roupa/acessório do próprio jogo (o servidor registra -> todos veem)
--   2) o que não conseguir pelo servidor entra só local (marcado F1L_Local)
--   3) a cada respawn tudo é recolocado e as juntas são soltas pra os acessórios seguirem o rig
-- Garantia total: deixe esses acessórios equipados no seu avatar do Roblox (aí já nascem no servidor).
local WEAR_KEYS = { "wear", "accessor", "hat", "avatar", "outfit", "catalog", "equip", "item" }

local function locustAppliedIds()
    local have = {}
    pcall(function()
        local hum = LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        for _, a in ipairs(hum:GetAppliedDescription():GetAccessories(true)) do
            have[a.AssetId] = true
        end
    end)
    return have
end

local function locustServerAccCount()
    local n = 0
    local chr = LocalPlayer.Character
    if chr then
        for _, a in ipairs(chr:GetChildren()) do
            if a:IsA("Accoutrement") and not a:GetAttribute("F1L_Local") then n += 1 end
        end
    end
    return n
end

local function locustWearRemotes()
    local list, seen = {}, {}
    local function add(r)
        if not seen[r] then seen[r] = true table.insert(list, r) end
    end
    pcall(function()
        local R = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Remotes")
        for _, n in ipairs({ "Wear", "WearItem", "WearAccessory", "Equip", "EquipItem" }) do
            local r = R and R:FindFirstChild(n)
            if r and (r:IsA("RemoteEvent") or r:IsA("RemoteFunction")) then add(r) end
        end
    end)
    for _, r in ipairs(ReplicatedStorage:GetDescendants()) do
        if r:IsA("RemoteEvent") or r:IsA("RemoteFunction") then
            local n = r.Name:lower()
            for _, key in ipairs(WEAR_KEYS) do
                if n:find(key, 1, true) then add(r) break end
            end
        end
    end
    return list
end

local function locustCallRemote(r, id)
    pcall(function()
        if r:IsA("RemoteFunction") then r:InvokeServer(id) else r:FireServer(id) end
    end)
end

function LOCUST.wear()
    local chr = LocalPlayer.Character or LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Wait()
    local hum = chr:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 10)
    if not hum then return false end

    local have = locustAppliedIds()
    local missing = {}
    for _, id in ipairs(LOCUST_ACCESSORIES) do
        if not have[id] then table.insert(missing, id) end
    end
    if #missing == 0 then
        notify("Locust", "Acessórios já estão no seu avatar (todos veem).")
        return true
    end

    -- 1) pelo remote do jogo
    local viaServer = 0
    local remotes = LOCUST.wearRemote and { LOCUST.wearRemote } or locustWearRemotes()
    for _, r in ipairs(remotes) do
        local before = locustServerAccCount()
        local first = missing[1]
        locustCallRemote(r, first)
        local t0, worked = os.clock(), false
        repeat
            task.wait(0.15)
            if locustAppliedIds()[first] or locustServerAccCount() > before then worked = true end
        until worked or os.clock() - t0 > 1.6
        if worked then
            LOCUST.wearRemote = r
            for i = 2, #missing do
                locustCallRemote(r, missing[i])
                task.wait(0.35)
            end
            task.wait(0.6)
            break
        end
    end
    have = locustAppliedIds()
    for _, id in ipairs(LOCUST_ACCESSORIES) do
        if have[id] then viaServer += 1 end
    end

    -- 2) o que faltou entra só local
    local added, failed = 0, {}
    for _, id in ipairs(LOCUST_ACCESSORIES) do
        if not have[id] then
            local ok, objs = pcall(function() return game:GetObjects("rbxassetid://" .. id) end)
            local acc
            if ok and objs then
                for _, o in ipairs(objs) do
                    if o:IsA("Accoutrement") then acc = o break end
                    local inner = o:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Accoutrement", true)
                    if inner then acc = inner break end
                end
            end
            if acc then
                acc:SetAttribute("F1L_Local", true)
                local done = pcall(function() hum:AddAccessory(acc) end)
                if not done or acc.Parent ~= chr then pcall(function() acc.Parent = chr end) end
                added += 1
            else
                table.insert(failed, id)
            end
        end
    end
    task.wait(0.8)

    if added == 0 and #failed == 0 then
        notify("Locust", "Acessórios: " .. viaServer .. " pelo servidor (todos veem).")
    elseif viaServer == 0 and LOCUST.wearRemote == nil then
        notify("Locust", "Nenhum remote de acessório do jogo achado: só VOCÊ vê. Pra todos verem, equipe esses acessórios no avatar do Roblox.")
    else
        notify("Locust", "Acessórios: " .. viaServer .. " servidor (todos veem) | " .. added .. " só local" .. (#failed > 0 and (" | falhou: " .. table.concat(failed, ", ")) or ""))
    end
    return #failed == 0
end

-- diagnóstico: mostra no console (F9) os remotes candidatos e o que está no personagem
function LOCUST.diag()
    local names = {}
    for _, r in ipairs(locustWearRemotes()) do
        table.insert(names, r.ClassName .. " " .. r:GetFullName())
    end
    local chr = LocalPlayer.Character
    local srv, loc = 0, 0
    if chr then
        for _, a in ipairs(chr:GetChildren()) do
            if a:IsA("Accoutrement") then
                if a:GetAttribute("F1L_Local") then loc += 1 else srv += 1 end
            end
        end
    end
    print("[Locust] remotes candidatos (" .. #names .. "):")
    for _, n in ipairs(names) do print("   " .. n) end
    print("[Locust] acessórios no personagem: servidor=" .. srv .. " | local=" .. loc .. " | remote usado=" .. tostring(LOCUST.wearRemote and LOCUST.wearRemote:GetFullName()))
    notify("Locust", "Diag: " .. #names .. " remotes | servidor " .. srv .. " | local " .. loc .. " (veja o console F9)")
end

function LOCUST.start()
    if LOCUST.running or LOCUST.busy then return end
    LOCUST.busy = true
    notify("Locust", "Iniciando...")

    local ok, err = pcall(function()

-- FEManager (baixa/gerencia arquivos) - cacheado entre execuções
LOCUST.fem = LOCUST.fem or loadstring(game:HttpGet("https://gist.githubusercontent.com/MelonsStuff/a003ea305dd302eab1f8d372daed38b4/raw/9db59962b28555fd699a7c29891efb85d45677ab/gistfile1.txt"))()
local FEManager = LOCUST.fem
if not FEManager then error("FEManager não carregou") end

-- tables
local ScriptOptions = {
	Settings = {
		AnimationSpeed = 1.25;
		Fling = true;
		Music = true;
		FOVEffects = true;
		CameraEffects = true;
		InfiniteJump = false;
	};
	Customization = { 
		HitboxColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255);
		Hitmarker = 103397225855135;
	};
	FlingOptions = {
		HatFling = false,
		Highlight = false,
		PredictionFling = true,
		Timeout = 0.5,
		ToolFling = false,
	};
	Reanimate_Settings = {
		Frequency = 6, -- this is basically how fast the oscillation goes
		Amplification = 6, -- this is how far the oscillation goes
		FrontOffset = 2.5, -- this is how much youre in front of the player during prediction
	}
}

local AnimatorCache = {
	Joints = {};
	AnimDefaults = {};
	Rigs = {};
}

local ScriptCache = {
	StoredMotors = {};
	TrashBin = {};
	HitHumanoids = {};
	ActiveConnections = {};
}

-- limpeza (usada no reset do personagem e no ;LOCUST_OFF)
local function Cleanup()
	for _, v in ipairs(ScriptCache.TrashBin) do pcall(function() v:Destroy() end) end
	for _, v in ipairs(ScriptCache.ActiveConnections) do pcall(function() v:Disconnect() end) end
	table.clear(ScriptCache.TrashBin)
	table.clear(ScriptCache.ActiveConnections)
	pcall(function() workspace.CurrentCamera.FieldOfView = 70 end)
end

local Directories = {
	Main = "FEVerse/",
	RBXMs ="FEVerse/RBXMs/",
	Sounds = "FEVerse/Sounds/",
	Songs = "FEVerse/Songs/",
}

local States = {
	Movement = {
		Idle = false;
		Walking = false;
		Running = false;
		Crouching = false;
		Sitting = false;
	};
	Input = {
		Debounce = false;
		Shift = false;
		KillMode = false;
	};
	Waypoint = {
		SavedPosition = nil;
		TVLocation = nil;
	}
}

local Services = {
	RunService = game:GetService("RunService");
	UserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService");
	TweenService = game:GetService("TweenService");
	Debris = game:GetService("Debris");
	Players = game:GetService("Players");
	InsertService = game:GetService("InsertService");
}

for i, v in pairs(Directories) do FEManager.EnsureFolder(v) end

-- baixa os arquivos se faltarem (antes de carregar o .rbxm, então não precisa rodar 2x)
local function haveFiles()
	if typeof(isfile) ~= "function" then return false end
	return isfile(Directories.RBXMs .. "Locust.rbxm") and isfile(Directories.Songs .. "LocustTheme.mp3")
end
if not haveFiles() then
	notify("Locust", "Baixando arquivos (só na primeira vez)...")
	FEManager.DownloadFile(Directories.RBXMs, "Locust.rbxm", "https://github.com/MelonsStuff/FEVerse/raw/refs/heads/main/RBXMs/Locust.rbxm")
	FEManager.DownloadFile(Directories.Songs, "LocustTheme.mp3", "https://github.com/MelonsStuff/FEVerse/raw/refs/heads/main/Songs/LocustTheme.mp3")
	if typeof(isfile) == "function" then
		local t0 = os.clock()
		while not haveFiles() and os.clock() - t0 < 20 do task.wait(0.25) end
	end
	notify("Locust", "Arquivos baixados.")
end

-- effects setup
local script = Services.InsertService:LoadLocalAsset(getcustomasset(Directories.RBXMs.."Locust.rbxm"))
local Animations = script:WaitForChild("Animations")
local Objects = script:WaitForChild("Objects")

-- reanim start
local Start, Stop
local Empyrean
do
	local Accessories = {}
	local Aligns = {}
	local Attachments = {}
	local BindableEvent = nil
	local Blacklist = {}
	local CFrame = CFrame
	local CFrameidentity = CFrame.identity
	local CFramelookAt = CFrame.lookAt
	local CFramenew = CFrame.new
	local Character = nil
	local CurrentCamera = nil
	local Enum = Enum
	local Custom = Enum.CameraType.Custom
	local Health = Enum.CoreGuiType.Health
	local HumanoidRigType = Enum.HumanoidRigType
	local R6 = HumanoidRigType.R6
	local Dead = Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead
	local LockCenter = Enum.MouseBehavior.LockCenter
	local UserInputType = Enum.UserInputType
	local MouseButton1 = UserInputType.MouseButton1
	local Touch = UserInputType.Touch
	local Exceptions = {}
	local game = game
	local Clone = game.Clone
	local Close = game.Close
	local Connect = Close.Connect
	local Disconnect = Connect(Close, function() end).Disconnect
	local Wait = Close.Wait
	local Destroy = game.Destroy
	local FindFirstAncestorOfClass = game.FindFirstAncestorOfClass
	local FindFirstAncestorWhichIsA = game.FindFirstAncestorWhichIsA
	local FindFirstChild = game.FindFirstChild
	local FindFirstChildOfClass = game.FindFirstChildOfClass
	local Players = FindFirstChildOfClass(game, "Players")
	local CreateHumanoidModelFromDescription = Players.CreateHumanoidModelFromDescription
	local GetPlayers = Players.GetPlayers
	local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
	local CharacterAdded = LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded
	local Mouse = LocalPlayer:GetMouse()
	local Kill = LocalPlayer.Kill
	local RunService = FindFirstChildOfClass(game, "RunService")
	local PostSimulation = RunService.PostSimulation
	local PreRender = RunService.PreRender
	local PreSimulation = RunService.PreSimulation
	local StarterGui = FindFirstChildOfClass(game, "StarterGui")
	local GetCoreGuiEnabled = StarterGui.GetCoreGuiEnabled
	local SetCore = StarterGui.SetCore
	local SetCoreGuiEnabled = StarterGui.SetCoreGuiEnabled
	local Workspace = FindFirstChildOfClass(game, "Workspace")
	local FallenPartsDestroyHeight = Workspace.FallenPartsDestroyHeight
	local HatDropY = FallenPartsDestroyHeight - 0.7
	local FindFirstChildWhichIsA = game.FindFirstChildWhichIsA
	local UserInputService = FindFirstChildOfClass(game, "UserInputService")
	local InputBegan = UserInputService.InputBegan
	local IsMouseButtonPressed = UserInputService.IsMouseButtonPressed
	local GetChildren = game.GetChildren
	local GetDescendants = game.GetDescendants
	local GetPropertyChangedSignal = game.GetPropertyChangedSignal
	local CurrentCameraChanged = GetPropertyChangedSignal(Workspace, "CurrentCamera")
	local MouseBehaviorChanged = GetPropertyChangedSignal(UserInputService, "MouseBehavior")
	local IsA = game.IsA
	local IsDescendantOf = game.IsDescendantOf

	local Highlights = {}

	local Instancenew = Instance.new
	local R15Animation = Instancenew("Animation")
	local R6Animation = Instancenew("Animation")
	local HumanoidDescription = Instancenew("HumanoidDescription")
	local HumanoidModel = CreateHumanoidModelFromDescription(Players, HumanoidDescription, R6)
	local R15HumanoidModel = CreateHumanoidModelFromDescription(Players, HumanoidDescription, HumanoidRigType.R15)
	local SetAccessories = HumanoidDescription.SetAccessories
	local ModelBreakJoints = HumanoidModel.BreakJoints
	local Head = HumanoidModel.Head
	local BasePartBreakJoints = Head.BreakJoints
	local GetJoints = Head.GetJoints
	local IsGrounded = Head.IsGrounded
	local Humanoid = HumanoidModel.Humanoid
	local ApplyDescription = Humanoid.ApplyDescription
	local ChangeState = Humanoid.ChangeState
	local EquipTool = Humanoid.EquipTool
	local GetAppliedDescription = Humanoid.GetAppliedDescription
	local GetPlayingAnimationTracks = Humanoid.GetPlayingAnimationTracks
	local LoadAnimation = Humanoid.LoadAnimation
	local Move = Humanoid.Move
	local UnequipTools = Humanoid.UnequipTools
	local ScaleTo = HumanoidModel.ScaleTo

	local IsFirst = false
	local IsHealthEnabled = nil
	local IsLockCenter = false
	local IsRegistered = false
	local IsRunning = false

	local LastTime = nil

	local math = math
	local mathrandom = math.random
	local mathsin = math.sin
	local mathpi = math.pi

	local nan = 0 / 0

	local next = next

	local OptionsAccessories = nil
	local OptionsApplyDescription = nil
	local OptionsBreakJointsDelay = nil
	local OptionsClickFling = nil
	local OptionsDisableCharacterCollisions = nil
	local OptionsDisableHealthBar = nil
	local OptionsDisableRigCollisions = nil
	local OptionsDefaultFlingOptions = nil
	local OptionsHatDrop = nil
	local OptionsHideCharacter = nil
	local OptionsParentCharacter = nil
	local OptionsRigTransparency = nil
	local OptionsSetCameraSubject = nil
	local OptionsSetCameraType = nil
	local OptionsSetCharacter = nil
	local OptionsSetCollisionGroup = nil
	local OptionsSimulationRadius = nil
	local OptionsTeleportRadius = nil
	local OptionsUseServerBreakJoints

	local osclock = os.clock

	local PreRenderConnection = nil

	local RBXScriptConnections = {}

	local replicatesignal = replicatesignal

	local Rig = nil
	local RigHumanoid = nil
	local RigHumanoidRootPart = nil

	local sethiddenproperty = sethiddenproperty
	local setscriptable = setscriptable

	local stringfind = string.find

	local table = table
	local tableclear = table.clear
	local tablefind = table.find
	local tableinsert = table.insert
	local tableremove = table.remove

	local Targets = {}

	local task = task
	local taskdefer = task.defer
	local taskspawn = task.spawn
	local taskwait = task.wait

	local Time = nil

	local Tools = {}

	local Vector3 = Vector3
	local Vector3new = Vector3.new
	local FlingVelocity = Vector3new(16384, 16384, 16384)
	local HatDropLinearVelocity = Vector3new(0, 27, 0)
	local HideCharacterOffset = Vector3new(0, - 30, 0)
	local Vector3one = Vector3.one
	local Vector3xzAxis = Vector3new(1, 0, 1)
	local Vector3zero = Vector3.zero
	local AntiSleep = Vector3zero

	local Color3fromRGB = Color3.fromRGB

	R15Animation.AnimationId = "rbxassetid://507767968"
	R6Animation.AnimationId = "rbxassetid://180436148"

	Humanoid = nil

	Destroy(HumanoidDescription)
	HumanoidDescription = nil

	local FindFirstChildOfClassAndName = function(Parent, ClassName, Name)
		for Index, Child in next, GetChildren(Parent) do
			if IsA(Child, ClassName) and Child.Name == Name then
				return Child
			end
		end
	end

	local GetHandleFromTable = function(Table)
		for Index, Child in GetChildren(Character) do
			if IsA(Child, "Accoutrement") then
				local Handle = FindFirstChildOfClassAndName(Child, "BasePart", "Handle")

				if Handle then
					local MeshId = nil
					local TextureId = nil

					if IsA(Handle, "MeshPart") then
						MeshId = Handle.MeshId
						TextureId = Handle.TextureID
					else
						local SpecialMesh = FindFirstChildOfClass(Handle, "SpecialMesh")

						if SpecialMesh then
							MeshId = SpecialMesh.MeshId
							TextureId = SpecialMesh.TextureId
						end
					end

					if MeshId then
						if stringfind(MeshId, Table.MeshId) and stringfind(TextureId, Table.TextureId) then
							return Handle
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end

	local NewIndex = function(self, Index, Value)
		self[Index] = Value
	end

	local DescendantAdded = function(Descendant)
		if IsA(Descendant, "Accoutrement") and OptionsHatDrop then
			if not pcall(NewIndex, Descendant, "BackendAccoutrementState", 0) then
				if sethiddenproperty then
					sethiddenproperty(Descendant, "BackendAccoutrementState", 0)
				elseif setscriptable then
					setscriptable(Descendant, "BackendAccoutrementState", true)
					Descendant.BackendAccoutrementState = 0
				end
			end
		elseif IsA(Descendant, "Attachment") then
			local Attachment = Attachments[Descendant.Name]

			if Attachment then
				local Parent = Descendant.Parent

				if IsA(Parent, "BasePart") then
					local MeshId = nil
					local TextureId = nil

					if IsA(Parent, "MeshPart") then
						MeshId = Parent.MeshId
						TextureId = Parent.TextureID
					else
						local SpecialMesh = FindFirstChildOfClass(Parent, "SpecialMesh")

						if SpecialMesh then
							MeshId = SpecialMesh.MeshId
							TextureId = SpecialMesh.TextureId
						end
					end

					if MeshId then
						for Index, Table in next, Accessories do
							if Table.MeshId == MeshId and Table.TextureId == TextureId then
								local Handle = Table.Handle

								tableinsert(Aligns, {
									LastPosition = Handle.Position,
									Offset = CFrameidentity,
									Part0 = Parent,
									Part1 = Handle
								})

								return
							end
						end

						for Index, Table in next, OptionsAccessories do
							if stringfind(MeshId, Table.MeshId) and stringfind(TextureId, Table.TextureId) then
								local Instance = nil
								local TableName = Table.Name
								local TableNames = Table.Names

								if TableName then
									Instance = FindFirstChildOfClassAndName(Rig, "BasePart", TableName)
								else
									for Index, TableName in next, TableNames do
										local Child = FindFirstChildOfClassAndName(Rig, "BasePart", TableName)

										if not ( TableNames[Index + 1] and Blacklist[Child] ) then
											Instance = Child
											break
										end
									end
								end

								if Instance then
									local Blacklisted = Blacklist[Instance]

									if not ( Blacklisted and Blacklisted.MeshId == MeshId and Blacklisted.TextureId == TextureId ) then
										tableinsert(Aligns, {
											Offset = Table.Offset,
											Part0 = Parent,
											Part1 = Instance
										})

										Blacklist[Instance] = { MeshId = MeshId, TextureId = TextureId }

										return
									end
								end
							end
						end

						local Accoutrement = FindFirstAncestorWhichIsA(Parent, "Accoutrement")

						if Accoutrement and IsA(Accoutrement, "Accoutrement") then
							local AccoutrementClone = Clone(Accoutrement)

							local HandleClone = FindFirstChildOfClassAndName(AccoutrementClone, "BasePart", "Handle")
							HandleClone.Transparency = OptionsRigTransparency

							for Index, Descendant in next, GetDescendants(HandleClone) do
								if IsA(Descendant, "JointInstance") then
									Destroy(Descendant)
								end
							end

							local AccessoryWeld = Instancenew("Weld")
							AccessoryWeld.C0 = Descendant.CFrame
							AccessoryWeld.C1 = Attachment.CFrame
							AccessoryWeld.Name = "AccessoryWeld"
							AccessoryWeld.Part0 = HandleClone
							AccessoryWeld.Part1 = Attachment.Parent
							AccessoryWeld.Parent = HandleClone

							AccoutrementClone.Parent = Rig

							tableinsert(Accessories, {
								Handle = HandleClone,
								MeshId = MeshId,
								TextureId = TextureId
							})
							tableinsert(Aligns, {
								Offset = CFrameidentity,
								Part0 = Parent,
								Part1 = HandleClone
							})
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end

	local SetCameraSubject = function()
		local CameraCFrame = CurrentCamera.CFrame
		local Position = RigHumanoidRootPart.CFrame.Position

		CurrentCamera.CameraSubject = RigHumanoid
		Wait(PreRender)
		CurrentCamera.CFrame = CameraCFrame + RigHumanoidRootPart.CFrame.Position - Position
	end

	local OnCameraSubjectChanged = function()
		if CurrentCamera.CameraSubject ~= RigHumanoid then
			taskdefer(SetCameraSubject)
		end
	end

	local OnCameraTypeChanged = function()
		if CurrentCamera.CameraType ~= Custom then
